Abstract: Nearest structured approximation and best structured preconditioning solve different matrix optimization problems. We determine their exact relation for Kronecker positive-definite matrices under the affine-invariant Riemannian metric. The Kronecker family is closed and geodesically convex, so every full matrix has a unique affine-invariant projection. Its logarithmic residual satisfies partial-trace normal equations and yields certified point and objective errors for an Armijo projection solver. Our central result shows that this unique projection is also a minimizer of the Hessian-relative condition number if and only if the extreme spectral states admit identical tensor marginals. A computable marginal-mismatch residual either vanishes at a condition-optimal projection or produces a strict descent direction. Two relative spectral levels always force projection optimality; more strongly, every $2\times 2$ Kronecker projection is condition-optimal. An explicit $2\times 3$ construction is therefore a dimension-minimal strict separation. Residual-calibrated bounds further bracket the best attainable Kronecker condition number and the suboptimality of the projection. Supporting results place classical diagonal and block Loewner sandwiches, fixed-basis primal--dual obstructions, and general log-spectral targets in the same certificate language. Given validated numerical enclosures and outward-rounded comparisons, an interval-safe corollary preserves the soundness of the full Kronecker tests. Deterministic small-matrix checks, including a multistart generic log-factor oracle independent of the partial-trace solver, verify the stated identities and bounds.

</details>

Abstract: Interior-point methods (IPMs) are widely adopted due to their high practical efficiency in solving linear, convex, and nonconvex optimization problems. For convex optimization, this performance is theoretically well-supported: there are strong complexity guarantees for self-concordant barrier setups \cite{nesterov1994interior}, which cover linear and conic optimization. On the other hand, unconstrained convex optimization is well-studied. However, there is limited analysis of constrained convex optimization methods without the self-concordance assumption. We develop and analyze a regularized Newton method with line search applied to the log barrier function in the setting that the objective and constraints are thrice differentiable and have Lipschitz continuous first and second derivatives. Starting from a strictly feasible point, our method finds an $ε$-approximately optimal solution in $\tilde{O}(ε^{-2/3})$ iterations.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化Shor松弛可能违反Slater条件的退化程度（即奇异度），并确定二元可行集的约束类型如何影响该指标。

Method: 理论分析：分别研究线性约束和二次等式约束定义的二元可行集，分析其Shor松弛的奇异度上界。

Result: 线性约束下奇异度≤1（0或1）；二次等式约束下奇异度可高达n（n为矩阵变量阶数减1）。

Conclusion: 线性约束情形奇异度有均匀上界，而二次约束情形奇异度可随问题规模线性增长，表明两类约束对Shor松弛的退化程度有本质不同的影响。

Abstract: Shor relaxations are standard tools for nonconvex quadratic optimization, but they may fail Slater's condition. The singularity degree quantifies this degeneracy by the number of facial-reduction steps needed to reach the minimal face. We determine how the constraints defining a binary feasible set affect this quantity. For every nonempty binary set defined by linear equations and inequalities, the Shor relaxation has singularity degree at most one. It is zero precisely when the linear programming (LP) relaxation contains a point in the open cube that strictly satisfies every inequality, and is one otherwise. As an algorithmic consequence of this characterization, Slater's condition can be restored by solving an LP rather than an auxiliary semidefinite program (SDP). In sharp contrast, for every $n\geq1$, we construct a binary feasible set defined by quadratic equalities whose Shor relaxation has a positive semidefinite matrix variable of order $n+1$ and singularity degree $n$. Thus, singularity degree is uniformly bounded in the linearly constrained case but can grow linearly with the matrix order when quadratic defining constraints are used.

</details>

Abstract: In this paper, inspired by the idea of Adaptive Gradient Descent (AdGD) by Malitsky and Mishchenko, we propose a method that adaptively provides a stepsize interval, which represents the condition for stepsizes ensuring global convergence. By projecting the Barzilai Borwein stepsize onto this interval, we can ensure global convergence and expect further acceleration of convergence. Furthermore, we propose enlarging the stepsize interval obtained by AdGD and show that global convergence can be guaranteed within this enlarged stepsize interval. This extension of the interval enables us to adopt the full Barzilai-Borwein (BB) stepsize more frequently. We report the results of numerical experiments on the stepsize that combines the proposed interval with the BB stepsize.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 理论证明了极端点几何可识别对偶代表点，使得广义雅可比矩阵非奇异；建立了全列秩条件与广义弱严格Robinson约束条件之间的关联；证明了非精确对偶SSN方法的局部超线性收敛性以及全局化版本的全局收敛性（最终恢复快速局部收敛率）。在正则化最优传输、电池调度可行性恢复、occupation-measure投影等数值实验中验证了方法的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的对偶半光滑牛顿方法有效解决了退化多面体投影问题中的雅可比矩阵奇异难题，无需显式正则化即可恢复牛顿步所需的正则性，且在理论和数值上均表现出良好的收敛性能。该方法为处理大规模退化约束优化问题提供了新的理论框架和实用算法。

Abstract: In this paper, we study dual semismooth Newton (SSN) methods for degenerate polyhedral projection problems, where generalized Jacobians of the dual residual may remain singular even arbitrarily close to the solution set. Rather than regularizing these singular systems, we exploit the nonuniqueness of the dual representation. We introduce a primal--dual lifted projection-equivalent set that always possesses extreme points without additional structural assumptions on the polyhedron, and show that its extreme-point geometry identifies dual representatives at which nonsingular generalized Jacobians of the dual residual can be constructed. This geometry is further linked to a full-column-rank condition and a generalized weak strict Robinson constraint qualification, showing that the regularity required by the Newton step can be recovered rather than imposed \emph{a priori}. We also establish displacement bounds that connect representative selection throughout the algorithm with the local Newton mechanism. Building on this variational framework, we develop an inexact dual SSN method with local superlinear convergence and a globalized version combining monotone representative selection with a Wolfe line search. The resulting method is globally convergent and eventually recovers the fast local rate. Numerical experiments on regularized optimal transport, battery-scheduling feasibility restoration, and occupation-measure projection demonstrate its robustness in highly degenerate settings.

</details>

Result: 在微电网能量管理问题上，与IPOPT和MadNLP相比，所提方法在保持可靠闭环控制性能的同时实现了显著的计算加速。

Conclusion: 学习加速的ADMM算法能够有效解决SBMPC的计算瓶颈，为不确定性下的实时控制提供了一种高效且准确的求解方案。

Abstract: Scenario-based model predictive control (SBMPC) is a variant of model predictive control (MPC) that explicitly accounts for uncertainty by optimizing control actions over multiple predicted scenarios. However, its computational complexity increases rapidly with the number of scenarios and prediction horizon, limiting is applicability to real-time planning and control. This paper presents a learning-accelerated Alternating Direction Method of Multipliers (ADMM) algorithm for efficiently solving SBMPC problems by leveraging parallel computing and Moreau envelope learning, while maintaining high solution accuracy. We reformulate the SBMPC problems into consensus forms that can be decomposed via ADMM, separating the scenario-dependent dynamics from non-anticipativity constraints and enabling parallel updates across scenarios and time steps. Building on this decomposition, we utilize existing learning-to-optimize schemes, which leverages Moreau envelope learning of the cost function to accelerate the primal update in ADMM, thereby reducing computation time. The proposed framework is evaluated on a microgrid energy management problem subject to load and renewable generation uncertainties. Comparisons with IPOPT and MadNLP, popular and modern nonlinear programming solvers, demonstrate substantial computational speedups while maintaining reliable closed-loop control performance.

</details>

Abstract: We characterize stability and its robustness for a class of boundary controlled, boundary observed hyperbolic partial differential equations. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Stiefel流形上的稀疏复合优化问题中，非光滑正则化器识别的活动流形与Stiefel流形可能非横截相交，导致无法直接应用黎曼牛顿方法进行加速。现有方法如ManPG收敛速度较慢，需要设计能处理这种几何障碍的牛顿加速算法。

Method: (1) 在横截情形下，证明了ManPG切向近端映射的局部识别性；(2) 针对非横截情形，引入离对角扰动的Stiefel族恢复识别几何，并给出O(‖Δ‖_F)-KKT保证；(3) 推导了可验证的支持级干净交集条件，覆盖非横截稀疏模式；(4) 提出MIX方法，结合保护的ManPG步和牛顿-CG步在移动识别交集上进行优化。

Result: 在干净交集设定下，MIX具有全局下降和KKT残差保证；在横截或一般扰动情形下，满足序列收敛和二阶充分条件时，能有限步识别活动流形并局部超线性收敛。压缩模态和稀疏PCA实验显示MIX大幅提升效率且保持解质量。

Conclusion: 本文系统地解决了Stiefel流形上稀疏复合优化的牛顿加速几何障碍问题，通过理论分析和算法设计，在保证收敛性的同时显著提升了计算效率，为带流形约束的非光滑优化提供了有效框架。

Abstract: We study Newton acceleration for sparse composite optimization on the Stiefel manifold. The main difficulty is geometric: the active manifold identified by the nonsmooth regularizer may fail to intersect the Stiefel manifold transversely, which obstructs a Riemannian Newton step on the identified manifold. In the transverse case, we prove local identification of the ManPG tangent proximal mapping. For nontransverse cases, we introduce an off-diagonally perturbed Stiefel family that generically restores the identification geometry while yielding an \(O(\|Δ\|_F)\)-KKT guarantee for the original problem. We also derive verifiable support-level conditions for clean intersection, which cover nontransverse sparse patterns and yield the smooth moving local models used by the Newton correction. Based on these results, we propose MIX, a safeguarded ManPG/Newton-CG method on moving identified intersections. In the general clean-intersection setting, we prove global descent and KKT-residual guarantees for MIX. In the transverse or generically perturbed cases, we further show that, under sequence convergence and second-order sufficient conditions, MIX identifies the active manifold in finite time and then converges locally superlinearly. Numerical experiments on compressed modes and sparse PCA show that MIX substantially improves efficiency while preserving solution quality.

</details>


### [78] [Host-Parasitoid Dynamics and Biological Control of the Sugarcane Borer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12878)
*Fredson Aguiar,M. Soledad Aronna,Roberto Guglielmi,Beatriz Laiate,Alexandre Molter*

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型未充分考虑卵期和幼虫期寄生蜂耦合作用以及寄生饱和效应，且缺乏对不同生物防治控制策略（特别是脉冲释放策略）的系统比较与优化。

Method: 构建六维寄主-寄生蜂动力学模型（Holling II型功能反应），进行平衡态分析与分岔分析，设计并比较开环最优控制、SDRE反馈控制和基于Lyapunov的脉冲反馈控制三种生物防治策略。

Result: 三种策略均能有效将害虫种群控制在经济损害阈值以下；脉冲反馈控制策略以显著更少的寄生蜂释放次数实现有效抑制，在田间应用中最具可行性。

Conclusion: 脉冲反馈控制策略是甘蔗螟虫生物防治中最具实际推广价值的方案，结合卵期和幼虫期寄生蜂的联合释放能实现可持续的害虫治理。

Abstract: We investigate biological pest control strategies for the sugarcane borer Diatraea saccharalis through the combined action of two parasitoid species: the egg parasitoid Trichogramma galloi and the larval parasitoid Cotesia flavipes. We describe the population dynamics with a six-dimensional host-parasitoid model in which host-parasitoid interactions are represented through a Holling Type II functional response, extending previous models by coupling egg and larval stage dynamics and incorporating parasitism saturation observed in laboratory experiments. We characterize the equilibrium structure of the model and analyze the local stability of the extinction equilibrium. Bifurcation analysis reveals that, for a wide range of Holling parameters, the pest population exceeds the economic damage threshold, motivating the design of active control strategies. We formulate and compare three biological control approaches: open-loop optimal control, State-Dependent Riccati Equation (SDRE) feedback control, and impulsive feedback control based on Lyapunov arguments. We perform numerical simulations to show that all three strategies successfully keep the pest population below the economic damage threshold. The impulsive strategy, in particular, achieves effective suppression with substantially fewer parasitoid releases than the continuous approaches, making it the most practically viable option for field implementation.

</details>


### [79] [Sharp Optimal Algorithm for Derivative-Free Stochastic Convex Optimization in One Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12938)
*Alexandra Carpentier,Chloé Rouyer,Alexandre Tsybakov,Arya Akhavan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对一维随机凸优化问题，在零阶带噪反馈场景下，提出了一种计算高效的算法，实现了最优的 O(1/√T) 收敛率，填补了已有上界与 Ω(1/√T) 下界之间的对数间隙，首次给出了该设置下的精确率保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在随机凸优化中，一阶反馈（梯度）下的收敛率已有成熟理论；然而在零阶优化（仅有带噪函数值）中，即使在一维情形，已知上界与下界 Ω(1/√T) 之间始终存在一个对数尺度的间隙，这促使作者试图填补这一理论空白。

Method: 提出一种计算高效的算法，针对定义域 [0,1]、值域 [0,1] 的凸函数 f，利用带 subGaussian 噪声的零阶 oracle（函数值查询）进行优化。

Result: 实现了 O(1/√T) 的最优收敛率，与下界 Ω(1/√T) 匹配，从而关闭了已有间隙。

Conclusion: 该工作在一维情形下首次给出了零阶随机凸优化的精确收敛率保证，消除了长期存在的对数差距。

Abstract: Stochastic convex optimization is a classical problem with well-understood guarantees under first-order feedback. In contrast, for zero-order optimization with noisy function evaluations, a logarithmic gap has persisted between known upper bounds and the $Ω(1/\sqrt{T})$ lower bound, even in the one-dimensional case. In this work, we study the problem of minimizing a convex function $f : [0,1] \to [0,1]$ using a zero-order oracle with subGaussian noise. We propose a computationally efficient algorithm that achieves the optimal $O(1/\sqrt{T})$ convergence rate, matching the lower bound. The result closes the existing gap in one dimension, providing the first sharp rate guarantee in this setting.

</details>


### [80] [Strict complementarity in semidefinite programming, singularity degree, and the (dis)connection of forward and backward errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12942)
*Gábor Pataki*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了半定规划（SDP）中缺乏严格互补性时，前向误差（真实误差）与后向误差（约束违反）之间存在的巨大差距。作者通过一种简单的规范形式刻画了严格互补性的缺失，该形式可通过初等行变换和旋转得到，并允许构造任意缺乏严格互补性的SDP。此外，论文精确刻画了奇异性度数与约束数量相等的条件，并通过大量计算实验发现前向-后向误差差距非常普遍，许多小规模SDP中前向误差比后向误差高出七个数量级，且两者呈负相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半定规划中严格互补性的缺失会导致内点算法的收敛行为异常，且前向误差（真实误差）与后向误差（约束违反）之间存在巨大差距，使得后向误差无法有效反映解的真实质量。因此需要系统理解和刻画这种差距的产生机制。

Method: (1) 提出一种简单的规范形式来刻画缺乏严格互补性的SDP，该形式通过初等行变换和旋转得到，使缺乏严格互补性变得明显；(2) 基于该规范形式设计了构造任意缺乏严格互补性SDP的生成算法；(3) 精确刻画了奇异性度数等于约束数量的条件；(4) 通过大规模数值实验（矩阵阶数≤20的小规模SDP）展示前向-后向误差差距的普遍性。

Result: (1) 给出了缺乏严格互补性SDP的规范形式及其参数化表示；(2) 严格刻画了奇异性度数与约束数量相等这一关键条件；(3) 数值实验表明前向-后向误差差距非常普遍，许多小规模SDP中前向误差比后向误差高出多达七个数量级；(4) 在某些数据集中前向误差与后向误差呈负相关，即真实误差越差越难被检测到。

Conclusion: 缺乏严格互补性会导致SDP的前向误差与后向误差之间存在显著且危险的不匹配，后向误差无法作为真实解质量的可靠指标。论文提供的规范形式和生成算法为研究这一现象提供了系统性工具，揭示了该问题的严重性和普遍性。

Abstract: Strict complementarity of a primal-dual pair of optimal solutions is fundamental in the numerical analysis of semidefinite programs (SDPs). Strict complementarity drives the convergence behavior of interior point algorithms. When it fails, pathological examples show a striking gap between two error measures of approximate solutions. The first of these is the forward or "true" error, i.e., the distance to the optimal solution set. The second is the less useful backward error, measured by the constraint violation.
  We first characterize the lack of strict complementarity in SDPs via a simple normal form. Our normal form has three key features: (i) it is obtained using elementary row operations and rotations; (ii) it makes the lack of strict complementarity evident; and (iii) it lets us construct any such SDP by a simple algorithm. A variant of our generating algorithm allows us to construct any SDP that fails strict complementarity but satisfies Slater's condition on both the primal and dual sides. Thus, we {\em parametrize} the data of all SDPs that lack strict complementarity in a manner similar to how the Jordan normal form parametrizes square matrices with given eigenvalue structure.
  Next, we precisely characterize when the singularity degree of an SDP equals the number of constraints -- a result that underlies our generating algorithms and that we believe is of independent interest.
  We construct and share a set of SDPs that lack strict complementarity and present a detailed computational study. We find that forward-backward error gaps are quite common: in many small SDPs (with matrix order $\leq 20$), the forward ("true") error exceeds the backward error by up to seven orders of magnitude. Further, in several data sets the forward and backward errors are {\em inversely} correlated. In other words, the worse the "true" forward error is, the harder it is to detect.

</details>


### [81] [Online Control via Counterfactual Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13029)
*Yunzong Xu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"反事实跟踪"的在线控制方法，能够与任意可测的因果策略类竞争（不仅限于线性控制器），在已知线性动态系统面对对抗性扰动和凸代价函数的情景下，达到极小极大最优的$\sqrt{T\log N}$遗憾界，并首次实现了对系统级响应球（无共同衰减包络、记忆长度或控制器阶数约束）的均匀控制保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线控制算法大多局限于线性控制器类，无法处理更一般的非线性、动态或非参数化策略。本文旨在突破这一限制，建立能够与通用因果策略类竞争的在线控制框架。

Method: 反事实跟踪方法：在每一轮，模拟基准策略在历史数据上的表现，利用其反事实状态-输入对构建移动参考轨迹，然后应用一个固定的稳定控制器在真实物理系统上跟踪该参考轨迹。

Result: 对有限规模$N$的策略类，达到$\sqrt{T\log N}$的极小极大最优遗憾界；对系统级响应球（有界脉冲响应增益偏差）的线性动态控制器类，首次给出均匀的在线控制保证，且匹配下界表明紧致性（仅常数差距）。

Conclusion: 反事实跟踪将在线控制从线性控制器推广到通用因果策略类，PAC-Bayes遗憾保证依赖于后验与先验的相对熵。对系统级响应球的保证是首个此类结果，且下界证明其最优性。

Abstract: We develop a method for online control that competes with general classes of causal policies, beyond the linear-controller classes used by most existing algorithms. Over a horizon of \(T\) rounds, we consider a known linear dynamical system subject to adversarial disturbances and convex costs revealed after each action. The method simulates the benchmark policies on the revealed history, uses their counterfactual state--input pairs to form a moving reference, and applies a fixed stabilizing controller to track that reference on the physical system. We call this method \emph{counterfactual tracking}.
  Counterfactual tracking applies to any measurable class of causal policies that can be simulated from the revealed history and whose counterfactual state--input pairs have bounded diameter at every round. The policies may be nonlinear or dynamic and need not share a parameterization. We establish PAC-Bayes regret guarantees that hold for every posterior over policies and depend on its relative entropy to a chosen prior. On a fixed plant with a tracker of bounded impulse-response gain, a finite class of \(N\) policies admits the minimax-optimal \(\sqrt{T\log N}\) dependence on \(T\) and \(N\) when \(\log N=O(T)\).
  As a central application, we compete with a system-level response ball of stabilizing linear dynamical controllers. The ball bounds the summed impulse-response deviation from the fixed tracker, but imposes no common decay envelope, memory length, or controller-order bound. To our knowledge, this is the first online-control guarantee uniform over such a class. A matching lower bound shows that our guarantee is tight up to constants.

</details>

### [82] [OmniPMNet: Bridging discrete and gridded PM10 forecasts via omni-query neural processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11896)
*Shuangshuang He,Shuo Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OmniPM-Net 是一个基于卷积条件神经过程（ConvCNP）的融合模型，通过地形感知高斯集卷积将图神经网络（GNN）站点预报提升到规则网格，与CAMS化学传输模型预报融合，在108小时预报范围内同时实现站点级精度和网格化空间场输出。基于2024年中国1,618个空气质量监测站全年数据验证，OmniPM-Net匹配了GNN基线站点精度（MAE 21.14 vs 22.00 μg/m³），并将CAMS的MAE降低30%，在高浓度尾部和沙尘事件中表现尤为突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 颗粒物（PM10）预报需要同时具备站点尺度精度和连续空间场，但化学传输模型（CTM）存在局部偏差，图神经网络（GNN）虽能精准跟踪站点却无法输出网格化结果，两者无法兼顾。

Method: 提出OmniPM-Net融合模型：1）地形感知高斯集卷积将不规则GNN站点预报提升到规则网格；2）多尺度空间源注意力（SSA）模块将网格化GNN预报与CAMS预报融合；3）共享全查询读取器将融合表示解码为站点或网格单元上一致的PM10预测。

Result: 在2024年中国1,618个监测站全年数据上，OmniPM-Net站点MAE为21.14 μg/m³（GNN基线22.00），CAMS MAE降低30%；90百分位MAE较GNN降低9%、较CAMS降低25%；沙尘事件中分类检测能力提升且能追踪空间轨迹。

Conclusion: OmniPM-Net成功弥合了站点级GNN预报与网格化CTM预报之间的鸿沟，在保持站点精度的同时提供了连续空间场，尤其在高浓度尾部和沙尘事件中优势显著。

Abstract: Forecasting particulate matter (PM10) requires both station-scale accuracy and continuous spatial fields, especially during severe dust storms. Chemical transport models (CTMs) provide gridded forecasts but retain local biases, whereas graph neural networks (GNNs) track monitoring sites well at short lead times but do not produce gridded outputs. Here we present OmniPM-Net, a Convolutional Conditional Neural Process (ConvCNP)-based fusion model that reconciles these two forecast types within a shared spatial representation. A terrain-aware Gaussian set convolution lifts irregular GNN station forecasts onto a regular grid, where a multi-scale Spatial Source Attention (SSA) module blends them with Copernicus Atmosphere Monitoring Service (CAMS) forecasts; a shared omni-query readout then decodes this representation into consistent PM10 predictions at either stations or grid cells over a 108 h horizon. Evaluated across 1,618 air-quality monitoring stations throughout China over the full year of 2024, OmniPM-Net matches the station-level accuracy of the stronger GNN baseline (mean absolute error 21.14 versus 22.00 ug/m3) and reduces the CAMS mean absolute error by 30%, while simultaneously delivering the gridded fields that the discrete GNN cannot. Its clearest gains are in the high-concentration tail, where the 90th-percentile MAE falls by 9% relative to the GNN and 25% relative to CAMS, and during dust episodes, where it improves categorical detection skill while tracking the evolving spatial trajectory.

</details>


### [83] [Semidirect Fourier Delta Attention: Phase-Controlled Delta Memory with Constructive Chunk-WY Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11897)
*Tiantian Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SFDA通过块旋转傅里叶控制替代实数对角衰减，在固定块内实现精确仿射块传输和低秩记忆，在循环状态追踪任务上显著优于KDA基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性注意力用固定循环状态替代softmax注意力不断增长的KV缓存，但这种压缩限制了精确状态追踪和长上下文记忆能力。

Method: 提出Semidirect Fourier Delta Attention (SFDA)，将实数对角衰减替换为块旋转傅里叶控制，引入相位参数θ_t的复对角矩阵Λ_t；并推导了构造性块WY分解，将乘积A_t...A_1分解为Γ_t - Y_t M_t W_t^*形式，确保秩增长被限制在固定块内。

Result: 代数上验证了该方法的正确性；在玩具状态追踪实验中，SFDA学会了循环记忆，而相位缺失的KDA基线几乎保持随机水平。

Conclusion: SFDA提供了精确的仿射块传输、形式化的稳定性和复杂度边界、以及相位加低秩记忆的紧凑刻画；融合内核和大规模语言模型比较留待未来工作。

Abstract: Linear attention replaces softmax attention's growing KV cache with a fixed recurrent state, but this compression limits exact state tracking and long-context memory. We introduce \emph{Semidirect Fourier Delta Attention} (SFDA), a phase-controlled generalization of Kimi Delta Attention that replaces real diagonal decay with block-rotational Fourier control: \[ S_t=(I-β_t k_tk_t^*)Λ_tS_{t-1}+β_tk_tv_t^*, \qquad Λ_t=\diag(α_t\odot e^{iθ_t}). \] Our main result is a constructive chunk-WY factorization for products \(A_t=Λ_t-u_tr_t^*\), giving \[ A_t\cdots A_1=Γ_t-Y_tM_tW_t^* \] with rank growth bounded inside fixed chunks. This yields an exact affine chunk transfer, formal stability and complexity bounds, and a compact characterization of phase-plus-low-rank memory. We verify the algebra numerically and show in toy state-tracking experiments that SFDA learns cyclic memory where the phase-disabled KDA baseline remains near chance. Fused kernels and large-scale language-model comparisons are left to future work.

</details>


### [84] [Repairing Shape-Prior Shortcuts in Long-Range Single-Shot Fringe Projection Profilometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11928)
*Adam Haroon,Cody Fleming,Beiwen Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对单次条纹投影轮廓术（FPP）中深度网络依赖形状先验捷径（从物体边缘而非条纹相位恢复深度）的问题，本文提出PhiCalNet：通过输出包裹相位表示$(\sinφ, \cosφ)$，并经由固定的可微标定层映射到深度，在架构层面消除形状先验解（而非通过损失惩罚）。在15600张合成条纹图像基准上，PhiCalNet将物体平均绝对误差从14.54mm降至4.46mm（3.3倍提升），误差集中于包裹相位$\pmπ$不连续处（仅0.103%像素）。同时，首次为FPP实现了逐像素共形不确定性量化，通过丢弃5%高分歧像素可将均方根误差降低64%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单次FPP网络存在严重的捷径学习问题：它们利用物体边界形状先验而非条纹相位来回归深度，且增加数据量或模型容量无法消除该问题，因为二者均未改变优化器搜索的假设空间。

Method: 提出PhiCalNet，其架构包含：(1) 输出包裹相位$(\sinφ, \cosφ)$的神经网络；(2) 固定的可微标定层将相位映射为深度，从架构层面阻止形状先验捷径。针对单次映射因缺少条纹阶次导致的非单射问题，将条纹阶次作为辅助输入（敏感性分析显示可容忍实际解码误差）。同时设计了物理信息神经网络（PINN）基线进行消融对比。

Result: 在15600张合成条纹图像（50个物体，1.5-2.1m距离）基准上，PhiCalNet将物体MAE从14.54mm降至4.46mm（3.3倍），误差仅0.103%像素位于包裹相位$\pmπ$不连续处。三帧扩展版本达到1.16mm。首次实现FPP的逐像素共形不确定性量化：丢弃5%高分歧像素可降低RMSE 64%（基线仅3.5%）。

Conclusion: 通过架构设计（而非损失惩罚）消除形状先验捷径是提升单次FPP深度估计精度的关键因素，包裹相位表示与可微标定层的结合在物理可解释性和不确定性量化方面均优于纯数据驱动方法。

Abstract: Single-shot fringe projection profilometry (FPP) networks that regress depth directly can exploit a shape-prior shortcut, recovering depth from object boundaries rather than from fringe phase. On a photorealistic synthetic benchmark (15,600 fringe images, 50 objects at 1.5-2.1 m standoff), the best such UNet baseline plateaus at 14.54 mm object mean absolute error (MAE), and neither more data nor more capacity removes the shortcut, because neither changes the hypothesis space the optimizer searches. We introduce PhiCalNet, which outputs a wrapped-phase representation $(\sinφ, \cosφ)$ and maps it to depth through a fixed differentiable calibration layer, removing the shape-prior solution architecturally rather than by a loss penalty. Because the single-shot mapping is non-injective without fringe order, PhiCalNet takes the fringe order as auxiliary input, an assumption a sensitivity analysis shows tolerates realistic decoding error; a physics-informed (PINN) baseline with the same physics as a soft penalty yields no gain, isolating the architectural choice as the operative factor. PhiCalNet reduces object MAE 3.3x to 4.46 mm, its residual confined to 0.103% of pixels at the $\pmπ$ wrap discontinuity, and a three-frame extension reaches 1.16 mm. Two checks agree: interpretability makes phase the most decodable internal feature, and pixel-wise conformal uncertainty quantification, to our knowledge the first for FPP, localizes error at the same discontinuity, where rejecting the top 5% of pixels by snapshot disagreement cuts root-mean-square error by 64% versus 3.5% for the baseline.

</details>


### [85] [Qubit-Efficient Quantum Search for Hyperdimensional Decomposition via Logarithmic Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11936)
*Sanggeon Yun,Hyunwoo Oh,Ryozo Masukawa,Raheeb Hassan,Mohsen Imani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向超维计算（HDC）分解的量子高效框架，通过将超向量表示成本从O(D)降低到O(log D)，并保持O(√(N^F))的搜索复杂度，实现了最高2000倍的量子比特节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的HDC分解需要搜索N^F个候选元组，计算成本极高。虽然近期量子方法提供了二次搜索加速，但依赖于O(D)量子位的超向量表示，量子比特效率低下。

Method: 1) 提出对数超向量（logarithmic hypervector）和绑定编码（binding encoding）；2) 设计可逆超向量查找算子（reversible hypervector lookup operator）；3) 结合改进的Dürr-Høyer搜索过程，将表示成本降至O(log D)，同时保持O(√(N^F))搜索复杂度。

Result: 实验验证了正确的相似度计算、在可执行范围内的准确分解，以及相比基于显式D量子位超向量编码的基线方法，实现了最高2000倍的量子比特数量减少。

Conclusion: 所提出的量子高效框架在保持搜索加速的同时，极大地降低了量子比特资源消耗，为大规模HDC分解问题提供了可行的量子解决方案。

Abstract: Hyperdimensional Computing (HDC) represents symbols using high-dimensional hypervectors of dimension $D$. In hypervector decomposition, the objective is to recover $F$ constituent hypervectors, each drawn from a codebook of size $N$, from a bound target hypervector. This requires searching over $N^F$ candidate tuples, making the task computationally prohibitive at scale. Recent quantum approach provides a quadratic search advantage, but typically rely on qubit-inefficient $O(D)$-qubit hypervector representations. We propose a qubit-efficient quantum framework for HDC decomposition that reduces the representation cost to $O(\log D)$. The framework introduces logarithmic hypervector and binding encodings, together with a reversible hypervector lookup operator for circuit-level manipulation of dense hypervectors. Combined with a modified Dürr-Høyer search procedure, the method preserves $O(\sqrt{N^F})$ search complexity while substantially reducing qubit usage. Experimental results validate correct similarity computation, accurate decomposition in executable regimes, and significantly improved qubit scaling over baselines based on explicit $D$-qubit hypervector encodings, achieving up to $2{,}000\times$ fewer qubits.

</details>


### [86] [Mirror Horizon: Viable Path Entropy as a Measure of Bounded Reflection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11937)
*Tiantian Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出通过**可行路径熵（VPE）**来度量智能系统在有限预算下的反复自省能力，将能力分解为可行延续概率与已验证延续模式的多样性。在GSM8K推理实验中验证，增加token预算能显著扩展可行可达性、降低零可达率、提高熵值，且Qwen2.5-1.5B在160 token下反射视野超过3B模型，表明能力不取决于参数量，而取决于有限反射协议下可访问的已验证延续容量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的智能系统评价仅关注单次准确率或pass@k等静态指标，忽视了系统在反复自我反思（reflection）下能否生成连贯、可持续的推理路径。作者希望建立一种新的度量框架，捕捉智能系统在有限预算下通过反复自省所能维持的已验证延续能力。

Method: 提出**可行路径熵（VPE）**，给定镜像状态、推演协议、验证器和模式映射，将有限预算下的能力分解为两部分：(1) 到达可行延续的概率；(2) 成功推演中到达已验证延续模式的多样性。理论层面重建了直觉（局部欠定约束）、品味（不变性选择压力）、反思（品味引导的欠定消解）、几何（使未来反思稳定的学习结构）四个概念。实验层面在GSM8K上使用Qwen2.5-Instruct系列模型，每问题32次采样、两种反射视野，比较96和160两种token预算下的表现。

Result: (1) 增加token预算从96到160显著扩展了已验证可达性，降低了零可达率，增加了已验证模式熵，并提升了平滑VPE；(2) 在160 token下，Qwen2.5-1.5B取得了最强的镜像视野，超越了参数更多的Qwen2.5-3B，表明镜像视野不等同于参数量；(3) 验证了能力是可行延续的可达结构，而非单次准确率或pass@k。

Conclusion: 支持**镜像理论**作为度量层面的解释框架：智能系统的能力应由其在有限预算反射协议下可访问的已验证延续容量来定义，而非静态指标。镜像视野（mirror horizon）是比参数规模更本质的能力度量。

Abstract: Mirror Theory proposes that an intelligent system should be studied not only by what it represents, but by what coherent continuations it can sustain under repeated reflection. We make this claim operational through \emph{viable path entropy} (VPE), a finite-budget measure of verified continuation capacity. Given a mirror state, a rollout protocol, a verifier, and a mode map, VPE decomposes bounded capability into two parts: the probability of reaching a viable continuation and the diversity of verified continuation modes reached among successful rollouts. This paper restores the full theoretical scaffold behind the measure: intuition as local underdetermining constraint, taste as invariant-selecting pressure, reflection as taste-guided resolution of underdetermination, and geometry as the learned structure that makes future reflection stable. We then instantiate the theory in language-model reasoning experiments on GSM8K. Across Qwen2.5-Instruct models, 32 sampled rollouts per problem, and two reflection horizons, increasing the token budget from 96 to 160 substantially expands verified reachability, reduces zero-reachability, increases verified-mode entropy, and improves smoothed VPE. At 160 tokens, Qwen2.5-1.5B realizes the strongest mirror horizon among the tested models, even though Qwen2.5-3B has more parameters. This shows that mirror horizon is not parameter count, but accessible verified continuation capacity under a bounded reflection protocol. The result supports Mirror Theory as a measure-level account: capability is the structure of viable continuations made reachable, not merely one-shot accuracy or pass@k.

</details>


### [87] [Mathematics of Data Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11938)
*Afonso S. Bandeira,Amit Singer,Thomas Strohmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本书系统介绍了数据科学的数学基础，涵盖高维数据分析、矩阵分解、线性回归、图与网络、降维、优化、分类、深度学习以及压缩感知等核心主题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为数据科学领域提供一个严谨的数学理论框架，帮助读者理解各类数据科学算法背后的数学原理。

Method: 采用教科书式的章节结构，从数学基础出发，依次讲解高维诅咒与祝福、奇异值分解与PCA、线性回归与正则化、图与聚类、非线性/线性降维、随机投影、优化方法、分类、深度学习、图拉普拉斯的大样本极限、测度集中、矩阵集中不等式、压缩感知与低秩矩阵恢复等内容。

Result: 构建了一个覆盖数据科学主要数学方法的完整知识体系。

Conclusion: 数据科学的数学基础是理解和设计高效算法的关键，本书为此提供了系统性的理论支撑。

Abstract: This book is about the mathematical foundations of data science.
  1. Introduction
  2. Curses, Blessings, and Surprises in High Dimensions
  3. Singular Value Decomposition and Principal Component Analysis
  4. Linear Regression and Regularization
  5. Graphs, Networks, and Clustering
  6. Nonlinear Dimension Reduction and Diffusion Maps
  7. Linear Dimension Reduction via Random Projections
  8. Optimization for Data Science
  9. Classification
  10. A Mathematical Introduction  to Deep Learning
  11. Large Sample Limit of Graph Laplacians
  12. Community
  13. Concentration of Measure and Gaussian Analysis
  14. Matrix Concentration Inequalities
  15. Compressive Sensing and Sparsity
  16. Low-Rank Matrix Recovery

</details>


### [88] [CARE-LoRA: Compressed Activation REconstruction for Memory-Efficient LoRA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11940)
*Gengyu Zhang,Haiyin Ran,Zhengbao He,Yuhang Liu,Hanling Tian,Zhehao Huang,Xiaolin Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CARE-LoRA提出了一种数据感知的压缩激活重建框架，通过利用LoRA固有的投影结构，用低秩压缩激活替代完整输入激活，并在前向传播中计算轻量级重建矩阵用于反向传播梯度信号重建，在显著减少内存开销的同时保持甚至提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大模型规模持续增长，在有限内存预算下进行微调变得越来越困难。虽然LoRA通过优化低秩适配矩阵大幅减少了可训练参数，但反向传播所需保留的激活值已成为LoRA微调中主要的内存瓶颈。

Method: CARE-LoRA利用LoRA的固有投影结构，用LoRA分支自然产生的低秩压缩激活替代完整输入激活；在前向传播中计算一个轻量级重建矩阵（额外计算成本可忽略），用于反向传播时重建梯度信号，从而保持LoRA矩阵的完全可训练性。

Result: 在多种模型和下游任务上的广泛实验表明，CARE-LoRA在显著降低整体内存占用的同时，达到了与标准LoRA及代表性LoRA变体相当甚至更优的性能。

Conclusion: CARE-LoRA是一种有效解决LoRA微调中激活内存瓶颈的方法，通过压缩激活重建框架在内存效率和模型性能之间取得了良好的平衡。

Abstract: As the scale of large pre-trained models continues to grow, fine-tuning them under limited memory budgets has become increasingly challenging. Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), currently one of the most widely adopted parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods, mitigates this challenge by optimizing only low-rank adaptation matrices, thereby greatly reducing the number of trainable parameters. With the parameter overhead substantially reduced, the activations retained for backpropagation have emerged as the primary remaining memory bottleneck during LoRA fine-tuning. To address this, we propose CARE-LoRA, a data-aware Compressed Activation REconstruction framework. By exploiting the inherent projection structure of LoRA, CARE-LoRA replaces the full input activation with the low-rank compressed activation naturally produced by the LoRA branch. It further computes a lightweight reconstruction matrix during the forward pass with negligible additional computation cost, which is used during backpropagation to reconstruct the gradient signal, thereby keeping LoRA matrices fully trainable. Extensive experiments across diverse models and downstream tasks demonstrate that, while substantially reducing the overall memory footprint, CARE-LoRA achieves competitive or even superior performance compared with standard LoRA and representative LoRA variants. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/fishandyu/CARE-LoRA .

</details>


### [89] [How Query Visibility Changes KV-Cache Compression Rankings: A Matched-Budget Audit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11942)
*Daming Luo,Christy Liang,Junyu Xuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对六种KV缓存压缩方法进行公平审计，发现在查询不可见（query-agnostic）协议下，压缩方法排名与常规的查询可见（query-aware）协议显著不同。大多数方法在查询不可见时表现不如简单基线（如保留开头和最近窗口），唯一例外是KeyDiff。方法性能下降幅度与其评分信号中查询可见程度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV缓存压缩方法大多在查询可见（query-aware）协议下评估，但这不符合实际部署场景——压缩应发生在查询之前（query-agnostic），以实现文档一次压缩、多次复用。因此需要在公平条件下重新审计各方法的真实表现。

Method: 对六种已发表压缩方法和三种简单基线，在三种7-9B模型上进行了匹配预算的审计（144,300对RULER-8192评估、40,800对LongBench评估、50,000次重采样配对bootstrap）。控制所有变量（模型、压缩比、实例、解码方式）不变，仅改变评分规则（查询可见 vs 查询不可见）。

Result: (1) 查询可见性改变排名：查询不可见协议下，五种共享注意力后端的审计方法中，仅KeyDiff持续优于最佳三选一基线（31/36单元）；最广泛部署的SnapKV平均落后于"保留开头和最近窗口"基线-0.066。(2) 两种协议间的性能下降幅度与各方法评分信号中查询可见程度一致：SnapKV下降最多（Delta=+0.198，因其64词观察窗口包含查询），KeyDiff下降最少（Delta=+0.011，其评分不含任何查询项）。

Conclusion: KV缓存压缩方法在查询不可见（实际部署场景）下的表现与现有评估结果大相径庭，提醒研究者和从业者应重新审视压缩方法的真实有效性，尤其需关注评分信号中查询的可见性对性能的影响。

Abstract: KV-cache compression methods are predominantly evaluated with the query appended to the context before compression -- a query-aware protocol. Yet the economic case for a compressed KV cache is reuse: compress a document once, answer many future questions against it. In that deployment, compression must happen query-agnostic -- before any question is seen. We present a matched-budget audit of six published compression methods against three trivial baselines on three open 7-9B models (144,300 paired evaluations on RULER-8192; 40,800 on LongBench; 50,000-resample paired bootstrap throughout). Everything is held fixed -- model, compression ratio, instances, decoding -- except the scoring rule. Three findings. (1) Query visibility changes the rankings: under the agnostic protocol, of the five audited methods that share a common attention backend, only KeyDiff beats a best-of-3 trivial baseline consistently (31 of 36 cells), and the most widely deployed method, SnapKV, loses to "keep the start and the recent window" on average (-0.066). (2) The per-method drop between the two protocols is ordered consistently with how visible the question is to each method's scoring signal, legible in its source code: from Delta=+0.198 for SnapKV (the question sits inside its 64-token observation window) down to Delta=+0.011 for KeyDiff (its score contains no query term at all).

</details>


### [90] [BattVAE-GP: Generative Modeling of Long-Horizon Battery Degradation with Uncertainty Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11943)
*Raghvender Raghvender,Mahdi Abid,Ferran Brosa Planella,Charles Delacourt,Arnaud Demortière*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种混合物理-概率学习框架（BattVAE-GP），通过VAE将电池退化轨迹编码到二维潜空间，再利用稀疏多任务高斯过程在潜空间内对未见过充电倍率下的退化轨迹进行连续插值和不确定性感知预测，显著降低了长时域物理仿真的计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时域基于物理的电池退化仿真计算成本高昂，限制了其在广泛运行条件下的密集探索，亟需一种计算高效且具有不确定性量化能力的替代模型。

Method: 1. 使用PyBaMM中的DFN/P2D电化学模型生成逐周期退化数据；2. 提取容量对齐的电压及导数特征，通过VAE编码到二维潜空间；3. 在潜空间中训练稀疏多任务高斯过程，以循环数和C倍率为输入，实现连续插值与不确定性估计；4. 解码潜状态得到电压-容量曲线，并通过潜空间到SOH的辅助预测器传播不确定性。

Result: 在协议级留出评估中，潜空间GP准确恢复未见过C倍率轨迹，不确定性行为与训练数据支持范围一致；在未见过内部C倍率下，生成轨迹在相邻协议间保持连贯位置；解码后的电压-容量曲线平滑，且通过蒙特卡洛传播获得不确定性感知的SOH估计。

Conclusion: BattVAE-GP框架为长时域退化建模提供了一种计算高效且具有不确定性感知能力的替代方案，为扩展至更丰富运行条件以及仿真-实验融合提供了结构化基础。

Abstract: Long-horizon physics-based simulations of battery degradation provide mechanistic insight but remain computationally expensive, limiting their use for dense exploration of operating conditions over extended cycle life. Here, we propose a hybrid physics-probabilistic learning framework for surrogate modeling of lithium-ion battery degradation trajectories at unseen charging rates. Cycle-resolved degradation data generated with a DFN/P2D electrochemical model in PyBaMM are first transformed into capacity-aligned voltage and derivative features and encoded using a Variational Autoencoder (VAE). The resulting two-dimensional latent space organizes degradation trajectories according to both cycle progression and charging protocol. A sparse multitask Gaussian process (GP) is then trained in this latent space using cycle number and C-rate as input variables, providing continuous interpolation of latent degradation dynamics together with posterior uncertainty estimates. Under protocol-level holdout evaluation, the latent-space GP accurately recovers unseen C-rate trajectories and exhibits uncertainty behavior consistent with the support of the training data. When queried at unseen interior C-rates, the model generates latent trajectories that remain coherently positioned between neighboring simulated protocols. Decoding the GP-predicted latent states through the frozen VAE decoder yields smooth voltage-capacity evolution, while Monte Carlo propagation of the GP latent posterior through an auxiliary latent to State of Health (SOH) predictor provides uncertainty-aware SOH estimates. The proposed BattVAE-GP framework therefore offers a computationally efficient and uncertainty-aware surrogate for long-horizon degradation modeling, providing a structured basis for extending battery health prediction toward richer operating conditions and future simulation-experiment fusion.

</details>


### [91] [Generalized Distribution-Free Semi-Supervised Learning with Risk Rewrite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11947)
*Yushi Hirose,Hiroo Irobe,Takafumi Kanamori*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于无偏风险估计的广义半监督学习框架，通过线性组合组件风险来构建估计器，突破了PNU学习仅限于二分类的限制，并推导出了最小可达方差，证明了在非对称损失场景下能获得更低的方差，进而建立了方差缩减与泛化性能提升之间的理论联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统半监督学习方法依赖于分布假设，当假设不满足时性能下降；而PNU学习方法虽然无分布假设，但仅限于二分类任务且方差最优性不明确。本文旨在提出一个更通用的框架，解决PNU学习的局限性。

Method: 提出广义框架，通过组件风险的线性组合构建无偏风险估计器，推广了PNU学习并扩展至多分类场景；推导了估计器的最小可达方差；建立了方差缩减与泛化性能之间的理论联系；基于理论洞见设计了两种实用的半监督学习方法。

Result: 理论方面：证明了所提估计器在非对称损失下可达到比PNU更低的方差，并建立了方差缩减与泛化性能提升之间的泛化界。实验方面：在二分类和多分类基准测试中，所提方法在经验上匹配或超越了现有方法。

Conclusion: 本文提出的广义无偏风险估计框架不仅理论上优于现有PNU学习方法（更低方差、更优泛化界），而且实践中在二分类和多分类任务上均表现出竞争力，为无分布假设的半监督学习提供了更通用的解决方案。

Abstract: Typical semi-supervised learning (SSL) methods rely on distributional assumptions, and their performance degrades when these are violated. While PNU learning, a risk rewriting method, offers a distribution-free alternative, it is restricted to binary classification and its variance optimality remains unclear. In this paper, we propose a generalized framework that constructs unbiased risk estimators using linear combinations of component risks, subsuming PNU learning and extending to multiclass classification. We derive the minimum achievable variance, demonstrating our estimator can attain lower variance than PNU in asymmetric loss scenarios. Furthermore, we establish a generalization bound directly linking this variance reduction to improved learning performance. Based on these theoretical insights, we introduce two practical SSL methods that empirically match or outperform existing approaches on binary and multiclass benchmarks.

</details>


### [92] [Are we Merging the Right Models? Impact of Expert Training Duration on Model Merging for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11997)
*Nikita Kozodoi,Zainab Afolabi,Jack Butler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了多任务模型合并中专家模型训练时长对合并质量的影响，发现不同合并方法对训练时长的依赖模式截然不同：简单平均法在过拟合时性能急剧下降，而基于稀疏化的方法在远超验证集最优点的训练步数下达到最佳性能。研究者通过偏差-方差分解分析解释了这一现象，类比随机森林中高方差个体学习器有利于平均的原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战多任务模型合并领域的一个常规做法——在专家模型达到最优验证损失时进行合并。作者认为训练时长与合并方法应联合选择而非独立决定。

Method: 在五个领域（数学、代码、指令跟随、多语言、安全）上微调Qwen 3.5三个规模（0.8B、2B、4B）的模型，保存从最优训练步数的25%到500%的检查点，评估五种合并方法在不同训练时长下的表现，并通过偏差-方差分解进行分析。

Result: 发现方法依赖模式：简单平均法在过拟合时性能急剧下降；稀疏化方法在远超验证集最优点的训练步数下性能最佳。

Conclusion: 训练时长和合并方法应联合选择，而非独立决定。通过偏差-方差分解将这种现象类比于随机森林中高方差个体学习器有利于平均的原理。

Abstract: Multi-task model merging combines separately trained expert models into a single model that handles all tasks without co-training. Standard practice merges experts at their optimal validation loss. We challenge this convention by systematically studying how training duration of domain experts affects the quality of the merged model. We fine-tune experts on five domains (Math, Code, Instruction Following, Multilingual, and Safety) across three model sizes (Qwen 3.5 0.8B, 2B, and 4B), saving checkpoints from 25\% to 500\% of the optimal training steps and evaluating five merging methods at each duration. Our findings reveal a striking method-dependent pattern: simple averaging degrades sharply with overfitting, while sparsification-based methods achieve their best performance well past the validation optimum. We formalize this through bias-variance decomposition analysis, drawing a parallel to random forests where averaging benefits from high-variance individual learners. These results suggest that training duration and merging method should be chosen jointly rather than independently.

</details>


### [93] [Scale-Aware Attention for Scarce Neural Data: An RG-Flow Transformer on Sleep-EDF EEG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11950)
*Dibakar Sigdel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文比较了具有重正化群（RG）归纳偏置的RG-Flow Transformer与参数匹配的vanilla Transformer在真实稀缺EEG数据上的睡眠分期性能。两者在5分类准确率上无显著差异（77.3% vs 77.0%），vanilla模型在数据稀缺场景下反而略微领先。但RG-Flow在可解释性上显著更优，能无监督地恢复出样本外的连续谱指数β（R²=0.416），这是vanilla模型不具备的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大脑场电位具有1/f^β的标度不变特性，谱指数β追踪皮层状态和睡眠深度。作者希望探究一个显式融入重正化群归纳偏置的Transformer（RG-Flow）是否能在真实稀缺EEG数据上相较于vanilla Transformer取得性能优势。

Method: 在PhysioNet Sleep-EDF数据集上，采用严格的逐受试者留出（无数据泄露）策略，进行5分类AASM睡眠分期评估。RG-Flow Transformer在标准自注意力之外耦合了一个尺度感知流（含可学习反常维度γ、块自旋粗粒化和熵门控同步桥）。对比基线包括参数匹配的vanilla Transformer和仅层次结构的消融模型。实验涵盖：(i) 5分类准确率基准测试；(ii) 逐受试者数据预算扫描以寻找归纳偏置交叉点；(iii) 检测RG-Flow学习的γ与实测谱指数β的样本外关联。

Result: 在5名受试者×5个种子的留一受试者交叉验证下，RG-Flow与vanilla Transformer的5分类睡眠分期准确率统计不可区分（77.3% vs 77.0%，配对p=0.294）。稀缺数据场景下未出现归纳偏置交叉，vanilla模型在所有数据受限预算下数值上均领先。但在可解释性方面，RG-Flow能样本外恢复连续谱指数β（R²=0.416），这是vanilla架构不具备的能力。

Conclusion: 引入重正化群归纳偏置的RG-Flow并未在睡眠分期任务上带来准确性提升，vanilla Transformer在数据稀缺时表现反而更好。然而RG-Flow提供了额外的可解释性维度——能通过学习到的反常维度γ无监督地追踪脑电信号的频谱标度特性，这为神经科学中的机制性建模提供了价值。

Abstract: Brain field potentials are scale-free: their power spectra follow a $1/f^β$ law whose aperiodic exponent $β$ tracks cortical state, and sleep depth in particular is a shift in $β$. We ask whether a transformer endowed with an explicit renormalization-group (RG) inductive bias -- the RG-Flow Transformer, which couples ordinary self-attention to a scale-aware stream with a learnable anomalous dimension $γ$, block-spin coarse-graining, and an entropy-gated synchronization bridge -- has an advantage over a parameter-matched vanilla transformer on \emph{real, scarce} EEG. Using the PhysioNet Sleep-EDF corpus with a strict leakage-free by-subject hold-out, we (i) benchmark RG-Flow against a param-matched vanilla transformer and a hierarchy-only ablation on 5-class AASM sleep staging, (ii) sweep the per-subject data budget to look for the inductive-bias crossover predicted when data are scarce, and (iii) test whether RG-Flow's learned $γ$ tracks the measured spectral exponent $β$ out-of-sample -- a quantity the vanilla model does not possess. Across $5$ subjects and $5$ seeds under leave-one-subject-out cross-validation, RG-Flow and the vanilla transformer are statistically indistinguishable on 5-class staging (77.3\% vs 77.0\% accuracy; paired $p=0.294$), and the predicted scarce-data crossover does not appear: vanilla is numerically ahead at every data-limited budget. What does separate the models is interpretability -- RG-Flow recovers the continuous spectral exponent out-of-sample ($β$-recovery $R^2 = 0.416$), a capability the vanilla architecture has no analogue for.

</details>


### [94] [Cluster-Weighted EDMD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12243)
*Lorenzo Tomaz,Judd Rosenblatt,Flavio Kicis,Thomas B. Jones,Diogo Schwerz de Lucena*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CW-EDMD方法，通过联合学习软相空间划分和每个簇的EDMD算子，在多个动力系统上显著优于标准EDMD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准EDMD使用单一全局Koopman算子，无法有效处理不同相空间区域具有不同局部动力学的系统。

Method: CW-EDMD通过期望最大化（EM）目标联合学习软相空间划分和每个簇的EDMD算子，其中每个转移的权重同时基于几何邻近性和预测残差，使得簇专门化于局部Koopman模型准确的区域而非数据密集的区域。

Result: 在Lorenz、阻尼摆和Duffing系统上，共36种配置和10个随机种子，CW-EDMD在一步预测和5秒滚动预测上均优于同阶EDMD。在288个配对比较中，258个案例误差显著降低，4个增加，26个无差异。中位数一步误差减少：阻尼摆57倍、Duffing 2.7倍、Lorenz 12倍。

Conclusion: CW-EDMD通过局部Koopman模型的专门化，能够有效处理多区域动力学差异，显著提升预测精度。

Abstract: Extended Dynamic Mode Decomposition (EDMD) approximates Koopman operators from data, but a single global operator is inefficient when different state-space regions exhibit distinct local dynamics. We introduce Cluster-Weighted EDMD (CW-EDMD), which jointly learns a soft phase-space partition and a per-cluster EDMD operator. Its Expectation-Maximization (EM) objective assigns each transition based on both geometric proximity and prediction residuals, so clusters specialize where local Koopman models are accurate rather than where the data are dense. On Lorenz, damped pendulum, and Duffing systems, across 36 configurations and 10 seeds, CW-EDMD improves matched-degree EDMD in one-step and 5s-rollout prediction. Across 288 paired comparisons, there are significant error reductions in 258 cases, increases in 4, and no differences in 26. Median one-step error reductions are 57x, 2.7x, and 12x on pendulum, Duffing, and Lorenz, respectively.

</details>


### [95] [Scalable Optimal Transport Algorithm for Network Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11952)
*Elaheh Hassani,Durga Mandarapu,Qi Yu,Hanghang Tong,Ariful Azad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FastAlign提出了一种基于稀疏感知的框架，通过重新解释最优传输计算为混合稀疏-稠密操作，结合自定义SpMM核与领域特定核融合，在不损失对齐精度的前提下，实现了CPU上3.89-9.45倍、GPU上2.24-32.54倍的端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于最优传输的网络对齐方法虽准确率高，但需反复构建和更新稠密矩阵，导致可扩展性差，难以处理大规模网络。

Method: FastAlign保留原始OT公式，将计算重新解释为一组重复的混合稀疏-稠密操作，结合稀疏感知图计算与领域特定核融合，包括自定义稀疏矩阵-稠密矩阵乘法（SpMM）核。

Result: 在CPU上实现3.89-9.45倍、在GPU上实现2.24-32.54倍的端到端运行时间加速，同时保持与最先进OT方法相当的对齐质量。

Conclusion: FastAlign通过稀疏感知优化有效解决了OT-based网络对齐的可扩展性瓶颈，在不牺牲精度的情况下大幅提升效率，验证了混合稀疏-稠密计算范式的有效性。

Abstract: Network alignment identifies node correspondences across different networks and is a fundamental primitive in many data science applications, including social network analysis, fraud detection, and knowledge graph integration. However, state-of-the-art network alignment methods often achieve high accuracy by repeatedly constructing and updating dense matrices, sacrificing scalability in the process. To address this scalability limitation without compromising alignment accuracy, we present FastAlign, a scalable, sparsity-aware framework for optimal transport-based network alignment. Rather than introducing a new alignment model, FastAlign preserves the original OT formulation and reinterprets its computation as a set of recurring mixed sparse-dense operations. FastAlign combines sparsity-aware graph computation with domain-specific kernel fusion, including a custom SpMM kernel. Our results show that FastAlign achieves alignment quality comparable to state-of-the-art OT-based methods while substantially reducing end-to-end runtime up to 3.89x-9.45x on CPU and 2.24x-32.54x on GPU.

</details>


### [96] [PolarBM: Complex-valued Boltzmann Machine for Modeling Audio Signals in Polar and Log-polar Coordinates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12417)
*Toru Nakashika,Kohei Yatabe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PolarBM和LogPolarBM，在极坐标系下对复数变量进行概率建模，显式建模幅度与相位依赖关系，其受限变体PolarRBM和LogPolarRBM在音频信号建模中精度优于传统模型及深度神经网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器学习方法处理复数数据时将其简化为实值变量，丢弃了幅度与相位之间的结构关系，无法捕捉复数信号中物理上重要的依赖信息。

Method: 提出PolarBM，在极坐标下定义复数变量的概率密度函数，使相位显式依赖于幅度；进一步提出LogPolarBM，在对数尺度上建模幅度，导出功率加权非中心复高斯（PW-NCCG）分布，其边缘幅度分布涵盖Rice、Nakagami和非中心chi分布作为特例。同时引入其受限变体PolarRBM和LogPolarRBM。

Result: 实验表明，通过显式建模幅度与相位依赖关系，所提RBM在建模精度上优于传统模型及深度神经网络。

Conclusion: 所提玻尔兹曼机不仅适用于音频信号，还可广泛推广至无线通信、量子力学等涉及复数数据的科学与工程领域。

Abstract: Although vast amounts of data, such as audio signal spectra, are naturally represented using complex numbers, conventional machine learning methods often simplify complex-domain problems by employing frameworks designed for real-valued variables. While this simplification offers computational benefits, it discards structural information regarding the inherent relationship between amplitude and phase. In this paper, we propose a novel Boltzmann machine (BM), named PolarBM, capable of naturally handling complex-valued variables in the polar coordinate (i.e., an amplitude-phase representation). PolarBM defines a probability density function for complex variables in which the phase explicitly depends on the amplitude, thereby capturing the physically important relationships of complex-valued signals. Furthermore, to process audio signals in accordance with human auditory perception, we propose LogPolarBM, which models amplitude on a logarithmic scale. This extension yields a flexible conditional probability density function, a power-weighted noncentral complex Gaussian (PW-NCCG) distribution, whose marginal amplitude distribution encompasses the Rice, Nakagami, and noncentral chi distributions as special cases. For practical applications, we also introduce the restricted variants of these proposed models: PolarRBM and LogPolarRBM. Experimental results demonstrate that by explicitly modeling the dependency between amplitude and phase, the proposed RBMs achieve superior modeling accuracy compared to conventional models, including deep neural networks. Although our experiments focus on audio signals, the utility of the proposed BMs is not limited to audio applications; their potential extends widely across various fields of science and engineering that involve complex-valued data, such as wireless communications and quantum mechanics.

</details>


### [97] [When Does Reward Teach State? A Hidden-Automaton Instrument and the Group-Language Boundary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11953)
*Jim Allchin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过将任务形式化为隐藏确定性有限自动机（DFA），使得智能体的奖励获取与潜在状态学习成为可独立测量的两个指标。研究表明，高奖励并不等同于任务理解，智能体是否学到潜在状态可通过优化器强度、任务结构和观测信息量三个轴预先预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习智能体获得高奖励是否意味着它学到了任务的潜在状态，还是仅仅利用了与奖励相关的捷径？由于"真实状态"通常无法定义，这一问题难以回答。本文旨在通过白盒工具使该问题变得可精确回答。

Method: 将任务表达为隐藏确定性有限自动机（DFA），智能体观察符号流并间歇性地在部分控制下选择下一个符号，最后获得稀疏的终止奖励。利用对自动机的已知信息，可免费获得最优回报（使奖励成为可解释的标准化分数）和每一步的精确潜在状态（从而在不暴露状态的情况下探测智能体的表征）。通过优化器强度、任务结构（置换/群语言结构）和观测信息量三个可控轴来研究奖励成功与潜在状态学习之间的耦合关系。

Result: 1) 弱策略RL下所有架构的智能体在状态探测上处于随机水平，但PPO+GAE可部分恢复状态（高种子方差）；2) 置换群语言结构可作为预警信号，在153个容量控制的自动机上以0.86的精度（89/103）单向预测感知缺口；3) 无标签辅助任务在观测不携带状态信息时无效，在观测揭示状态时按比例恢复。

Conclusion: 高奖励不是任务理解的证据；智能体是否恢复潜在状态是可预先预测的。该方法可区分感知缺口（潜在状态无法线性恢复但可表征）与规划缺口（状态可恢复但未被利用），这是仅依赖奖励评估无法实现的区分。

Abstract: Does a reinforcement-learning agent that earns high reward represent its task's latent state, or only a reward-correlated shortcut? The question is usually unanswerable: the "true state" is undefined. We make it exactly answerable with a white-box instrument: express the task as a hidden deterministic finite automaton (DFA), let the agent observe a symbol stream and intermittently choose the next symbol under partial control, and grant one sparse terminal reward for acceptance. Knowing the automaton gives two things for free: the optimal return (so reward becomes an interpretable normalized score) and the exact latent state at every step (so we can probe the agent's representation without ever showing it). Reward success and latent-state learning thus become separately measured quantities whose coupling is governed by three controllable axes. Optimizer strength: under weak on-policy RL the agent earns reward with the state probe at chance for every architecture, tempting the conclusion that sparse RL cannot install latent state; a pre-registered control overturns it -- PPO+GAE recovers the state, but only partially and with high seed variance. Task structure: permutation (group-language) structure is a warning sign computable from the transition function before any training, and held out on 153 capacity-controlled fresh automata it flags perception gaps at precision 0.86 (89 of 103), in one direction only. Observation informativeness: a label-free auxiliary is vacuous when observations carry no state and recovers it in proportion to how much they reveal. The payoff is a distinction reward-only evaluation cannot make: a perception gap (latent state not linearly recoverable, though representable) versus a planning gap (state recoverable but unused). High reward is thus not evidence of task understanding; whether an agent recovers latent state is predictable in advance.

</details>


### [98] [Fisher Rank Inflation: A Spectral Signature of Memorization under Label Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12438)
*Satwik Bathula,Anand A. Joshi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Fisher秩膨胀现象，即网络在记忆噪声标签时，最后一层梯度协方差矩阵的有效秩会先扩张后收缩，可作为检测标签噪声的谱特征。实验表明该现象与记忆化进程对齐，能有效识别 corrupted 样本，且秩膨胀的起始点早于测试性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度网络在含噪标签训练中会先学习干净结构再记忆错误标签，现有方法缺乏对这一过渡过程的谱级理解。本文旨在通过谱分析揭示噪声标签记忆化的内在机制，并建立可检测的谱信号。

Method: 通过分析每个样本最后一层梯度的中心化散度矩阵的谱特性，定义Fisher有效秩；推导一阶留一法归因公式，识别corrupted样本的谱贡献条件；在CIFAR系列数据集上使用SmallCNN、ResNet18和ViT进行验证。

Result: Fisher有效秩呈现先膨胀后收缩的轨迹，与记忆化进程对齐。峰值处corrupted样本在最高贡献样本中占比达69.2%-96.2%；有效秩随噪声率单调增长（28.88±1.95至97.09±1.78）；秩膨胀起始点早于测试性能下降。

Conclusion: Fisher秩膨胀是连接corrupted样本富集、噪声严重程度以及从结构学习到记忆化过渡的谱级信号，为理解噪声标签下的网络行为提供了新的谱分析视角。

Abstract: Deep networks trained with label noise often learn clean structure before memorizing corrupted labels. We show that this transition leaves a spectral signature in the centered scatter of per-example last-layer gradients. Its effective rank transiently expands during memorization and contracts after corrupted labels are fit. We call this phenomenon Fisher Rank Inflation. Corrupted labels increase effective rank by injecting spectral mass into low-energy or previously unused eigendirections, increasing the entropy of the gradient spectrum. We derive a first-order leave-one-out attribution formula, identify conditions under which corrupted examples contribute more strongly than clean examples, and explain why attribution signals weaken once the normalized Fisher-gradient spectrum stabilizes. We test these predictions on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and CIFAR-10N using SmallCNN, ResNet18, and Vision Transformers. Across settings, Fisher effective rank exhibits a consistent inflation--collapse trajectory aligned with memorization. At peak-rank checkpoints, corrupted examples are enriched among the highest rank-contributing samples, with top-100 noisy fractions from \(69.2\%\) to \(96.2\%\) across five-seed synthetic-corruption experiments and \(94.4\%\pm1.9\%\) on CIFAR-10N. First-order spectral attribution closely matches exact leave-one-out contributions in convolutional models and remains enriched in the Vision Transformer. Peak effective rank increases monotonically with corruption severity, from \(28.88\pm1.95\) under clean training to \(97.09\pm1.78\) at \(60\%\) corruption. In several settings, the retrospectively identified onset of rank inflation precedes observable test degradation. These results establish Fisher Rank Inflation as a spectral signature connecting corrupted-example enrichment, corruption severity, and the transition from structure learning to memorization.

</details>


### [99] [Graph-Constrained Policy Learning for Extreme Clinical Code Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11954)
*Amritpal Singh,Sebastian Torres,Khawar Shakeel,Syed Ahmad Chan Bukhari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种图约束遍历策略（Graph-Constrained Traversal Policy），将ICD代码预测建模为有限时域决策过程，通过单个语言模型沿代码层级逐级选择子节点直至叶节点，将极端多标签分类转化为稀疏、层级感知的子集决策。在MIMIC-IV数据集上，该监督策略SFT-1+在15,761个全量代码空间中达到0.527 micro-F1，超过CAML、LAAT、PLM-ICD等基线方法，尤其在罕见代码预测上提升显著，且避免了三专家级联模型的上下文窗口溢出问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ICD代码预测方法将任务视为扁平多标签分类，独立打分导致罕见标签训练信号不足，且无法保证输出结构的合法性。作者希望利用代码层级结构，将极端多标签预测转化为层级感知的决策过程，以缓解罕见代码瓶颈并保证输出结构有效。

Method: 提出图约束遍历策略，将ICD预测建模为有限时域马尔可夫决策过程。单个语言模型作为策略网络，从根节点开始沿修剪后的代码层级图逐级下行，每一步选择有效的子节点，直至到达可计费的叶节点。训练采用监督微调（SFT）和GRPO强化学习，并通过受控析因实验评估架构、训练算法和数据预算的影响。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV上，SFT-1+在50代码子集上micro-F1为0.709，在全量15,761代码空间上micro-F1为0.527，优于CAML、LAAT、PLM-ICD等扁平基线。全量设置下较最强扁平基线提升0.044 micro-F1和0.157 macro-F1。单个策略模型匹配三专家级联性能，且避免后者在28-32%测试样本上的上下文溢出问题。增加监督轨迹数据是唯一稳定提升性能的干预，GRPO强化学习无额外收益。

Conclusion: 简单的图约束策略学习能够在极端临床代码预测任务上超越更复杂的扁平、级联和强化学习替代方案，有效缓解罕见代码预测瓶颈并保证输出结构合法性。

Abstract: Clinical code prediction maps unstructured discharge summaries to ICD-10-CM leaf codes in a large, sparse, and deeply hierarchical label space. Most systems treat the task as flat multi-label classification, scoring codes independently and providing limited training signal for rare labels. We propose a graph-constrained traversal policy that formulates ICD prediction as a finite-horizon decision process over a pruned code hierarchy. A single language model descends the graph level by level, selecting valid child nodes until billable leaf codes are reached. This converts extreme multi-label prediction into sparse, hierarchy-aware subset decisions while guaranteeing structurally valid outputs.
  On MIMIC-IV discharge summaries, our best supervised policy, SFT-1+, achieves 0.709 micro-F1 on a curated 50-code subset and 0.527 micro-F1 on the full 15,761-code space, outperforming flat baselines including CAML, LAAT, and PLM-ICD. In the full setting, SFT-1+ improves over the strongest flat baseline by 0.044 micro-F1 and 0.157 macro-F1, suggesting that graph-constrained decomposition mitigates the rare-code bottleneck. A controlled factorial study evaluates architecture, training algorithm, and data budget. Across both scales, one shared policy matches a three-specialist cascade while avoiding its context-window overflow on 28-32% of full-space test notes. Increasing supervised trajectory data is the only intervention that consistently improves performance, while GRPO reinforcement learning provides no benefit over supervised continuation with matched data. These results show that simple graph-constrained policy learning can outperform more complex flat, cascaded, and reinforcement-learning alternatives for extreme clinical code prediction.

</details>


### [100] [What Does Goodness Measure? A Likelihood-Ratio Account of Forward-Forward Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12501)
*Paolo Giannitrapani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从显式生成模型的角度重新解释了Forward-Forward算法：平方 goodness 是两个零均值、尺度不同的总体之间似然比检验的充分统计量，FF阈值是其决策边界。该视角推广到各向异性分布（马氏距离）、重尾分布（饱和统计量，斜率是后验精度即除性归一化）等情况。层间归一化必须去除长度而保留每坐标能量，否则导致深度坍塌；成对目标存在尺度膨胀捷径，白化 goodness 可消除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Forward-Forward算法中平方 goodness、层间归一化等设计通常被视为启发式方法，缺乏理论支撑。本文旨在通过显式生成模型为这些选择提供统计理论基础。

Method: 将FF算法置于显式生成模型框架下，利用似然比检验、马氏距离、后验概率等统计概念重新解释平方 goodness、阈值、层间归一化等组件，并分析各向异性、重尾分布等推广情形。

Result: 平方 goodness 是零均值异尺度总体间似然比检验的充分统计量；各向异性需用马氏距离；重尾分布下 goodness 饱和，斜率为后验精度（除性归一化）；层间归一化需保留每坐标能量而非单位范数，否则深度坍塌；成对目标存在尺度膨胀问题，白化可消除。

Conclusion: FF算法中的启发式设计本质上对应于显式生成模型下的最优统计决策，为理解和改进FF算法提供了理论基础。

Abstract: The Forward-Forward (FF) algorithm trains each layer locally, so that a scalar goodness - the sum of squared activations - is high on real inputs and low on contrastive ones, with activations normalized between layers. Both choices are usually treated as heuristics. Under an explicit generative model they are not: the squared goodness is the sufficient statistic of a likelihood-ratio test between two zero-mean populations differing in scale, and the FF threshold is its boundary. It generalizes: anisotropic populations yield a Mahalanobis goodness, the plain square being its isotropic case; heavy-tailed populations yield a saturating statistic whose slope is a posterior precision - divisive normalization - with bounded evidence and an advantage only under aggregation. The same lens characterizes the inter-layer normalization: it must remove the length while preserving per-coordinate energy, explaining a depth collapse we observe under unit-norm normalization; and the pairwise objective admits a scale-inflation shortcut that a whitened goodness removes.

</details>


### [101] [Exact and Certified Data Shapley for Weighted k-Nearest-Neighbor Regression and Soft-Label Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11956)
*Zongye Lyu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个针对加权KNN回归Data Shapley的伪多项式精确算法和全精度FPTAS，填补了该领域长期存在的理论空白，并实验验证了精确值在确定性、可认证误差边界和审计估计器方面的优势，而蒙特卡洛方法无法重现精确排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Data Shapley是量化训练数据点价值的标准理论框架，其KNN特化版本已被实际部署（如pyDVL、OpenDataVal）。然而，加权KNN回归和软标签预测的精确计算一直存在空白：现有唯一精确方法是O(N^K)暴力枚举，指数于邻域大小K。根本障碍在于加权回归预测是两项联盟依赖比值的和，其归一化分母破坏了先前多项式算法依赖的加法、阈值和复制结构。填补这一理论空白是本文的核心动机。

Method: (i) 提出首个伪多项式精确算法：基于联合整数状态(权重和, 权重与目标乘积和)的计数动态规划，在固定格点精度下多项式于N和K；(ii) 针对连续权重和目标设计全精度FPTAS（完全多项式时间近似方案），提供可机器检查的逐值误差证书；(iii) 建立复杂度全景图，包括无条件Omega(D_w)输出规模下界和访问模型困难性结果；(iv) 将方法扩展到加权软标签多类分类场景。

Result: 精确算法在12,716个对抗性实例上与穷举枚举零偏差验证；FPTAS在86,400次检查中从未违反误差证书。在误标签检测下游任务中，精确值与蒙特卡洛Data Shapley在数据集层面统计等价（TOST, n=8, p<10^-4），但蒙特卡洛方法在任何预算下（最多3,000次排列，约1.28e6次效用评估）均无法复现精确的前10%排名。

Conclusion: 本文首次解决了加权KNN回归Data Shapley的精确计算问题，提供了理论算法和开源实现。精确值的核心价值不在于统计性能提升（与蒙特卡洛统计等价），而在于确定性、可认证误差边界以及作为审计估计器的精确参考标准。

Abstract: Data Shapley is the standard principled answer to which training points are worth what, and its k-nearest-neighbor (KNN) specialization is the version deployed in practice: the exact estimator shipped by toolkits such as pyDVL and OpenDataVal. Exact algorithms are known for unweighted KNN and for weighted KNN classification, but weighted KNN regression and soft-label prediction have resisted: the only exact method is an O(N^K) brute force, exponential in neighborhood size K. The obstruction: the weighted regression prediction is a ratio of two coalition-dependent sums, whose normalization denominator breaks the additive, threshold, and duplication structures the prior polynomial algorithms rely on. We close this gap. We give (i) the first pseudo-polynomial-time exact algorithm (polynomial in N and K at fixed lattice precision) for weighted KNN-regression Data Shapley, a counting dynamic program over the joint integer state (sum of w, sum of w*y), verified against exhaustive enumeration with zero mismatch on 12,716 adversarial instances; (ii) a certified FPTAS for continuous weights and targets, with a machine-checkable per-value error certificate never violated across 86,400 checks; (iii) a complexity landscape, including an unconditional Omega(D_w) output-size lower bound and access-model hardness results; and (iv) a weighted soft-label multi-class extension. We release an open-source, CPU-only library and the first exact weighted-regression Data Shapley ground truth. On downstream mislabel detection our exact values are statistically equivalent to Monte-Carlo Data Shapley (dataset-level TOST, n=8, p<10^-4), the pre-registered outcome; the value of exactness is instead determinism, a certified error bound, and an exact reference for auditing estimators: Monte-Carlo did not reproduce the exact top-10% ranking at any budget tested, up to 3,000 permutations (~1.28e6 utility evaluations).

</details>


### [102] [A JoLT for the KV Cache: Near-Lossless KV Cache Compression via Joint Tucker and JL-Residual Allocation for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12550)
*Rahul Krishnan,Volker Schulz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: JoLT是一种针对Transformer推理中KV缓存的三阶张量压缩方法，通过部分Tucker分解（压缩token和特征轴）结合JL旋转低比特残差，在单字节预算下用Lagrangian对偶分配Tucker秩和残差比特宽度，实现近无损的2-3倍压缩，在Mistral-7B和LLaMA-2-13B上保持困惑度、GSM8K准确率和RULER检索与未压缩基线无统计差异，相对Frobenius误差比跨层SVD和4比特量化低约一个数量级。FlashJoLT变体提供5-13倍压缩加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KV缓存是Transformer推理的主要内存瓶颈，随批量大小、上下文长度和深度增长，在长上下文场景中限制吞吐量。现有低秩方法和量化方法分别处理二维切片或降低比特宽度，但都未利用缓存天然是三阶张量（头轴、token轴、特征轴）且各轴冗余度不同的特性。

Method: 将KV缓存视为三阶张量（头×token×特征），应用部分Tucker分解仅压缩token和特征轴，保留头和层轴完整；然后用JL旋转低比特残差恢复截断能量；通过单个Lagrangian对偶在每层组内联合优化Tucker秩和残差比特宽度，分别针对键和值，在单字节预算下分配。

Result: 在Mistral-7B-v0.3（分组查询注意力）和LLaMA-2-13B（多头注意力）上实现近无损2-3倍压缩：困惑度、GSM8K准确率、RULER检索与基线无统计差异；2倍压缩时相对Frobenius误差K为0.009、V为0.006，比跨层SVD和4比特量化低约一个数量级；FlashJoLT变体实现5-13倍压缩加速。

Conclusion: 通过显式利用KV缓存的三阶张量结构，联合优化Tucker分解和残差量化，在极低误差下实现显著压缩，优于仅考虑二维或量化方法。

Abstract: The key-value (KV) cache has become the dominant memory cost of transformer inference. It grows with batch size, context length, and depth, and at long context it, rather than the model weights, sets the ceiling on throughput. Two families of methods reduce it. Low-rank methods factor two-dimensional slices of the cache, either per-head matrices or cross-layer feature blocks, and quantization methods lower the bit-width of every entry. Neither family exploits the fact that the cache at a layer is naturally a third-order tensor whose three axes, the heads, the tokens, and the features, carry very different amounts of redundancy. We take this tensor view directly. Our method, JoLT, applies a partial Tucker decomposition that compresses only the token and feature axes while leaving the head and layer axes intact, and then restores the energy that truncation discards with a Johnson-Lindenstrauss (JL) rotated low-bit residual. A single Lagrangian dual allocates the Tucker ranks and the residual bit-widths together, per layer group and separately for keys and values, under one byte budget. The result is a near-lossless 2-3x compression: perplexity, GSM8K accuracy, and RULER needle-in-a-haystack retrieval all stay at or within statistical noise of the uncompressed baseline on both a grouped-query-attention model (Mistral-7B-v0.3) and a multi-head-attention model (LLaMA-2-13B). At 2x, JoLT reconstructs the cache to relative Frobenius error 0.009 (K) and 0.006 (V) on both architectures, roughly an order of magnitude below cross-layer SVD and 4-bit quantization. A randomized-SVD variant, FlashJoLT, delivers a 5-13x compression-time speedup at matched quality.

</details>


### [103] [Constructed Reality, Contested Priors: Decoupling and the Architecture of Cognitive Relapse Under the Free Energy Principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11958)
*MD Ibrahim Hossain Ridoy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过卷积变分自编码器与循环潜在预测器作为自由能原理的代理，证明了“本体论反转”的计算可行性：系统表征能力与默认行为可分离，且存在“认知复发”这一结构性故障模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自由能原理下，预测系统不直接观察现实，而是维持一个生成模型并体验其当前最佳假设。本研究试图探究是否可能构建一个足够一致的合成环境，使预测系统的推理机制将其采纳为默认假设，从而永久取代最初塑造它的环境——这一状态被称为“本体论反转”。由于在神经系统中诱导和监测这种转变既不合伦理也不技术可行，因此通过计算代理来研究其底层计算问题。

Method: 使用卷积变分自编码器（VAE）配以循环潜在预测器作为代理系统，其证据下界（ELBO）目标在数学上与变分自由能仅相差一个符号。网络先在基线视觉域上训练，然后进入混合流阶段，其中通过清扫式排练比率r控制从基线域到目标域过渡期间基线内容的保留量。分别追踪表征能力（潜在空间能区分什么）和默认行为（系统在无约束时生成什么）。共进行90次全范围清扫实验。

Result: 表征能力与默认行为出现显著分离：无论r值如何，表征精度始终接近天花板（0.97至0.998），而默认行为完全由r决定，跨越系统几乎整个范围，实现了学习与接受的解耦。更引人注目的是，在中间r值下，系统的默认输出先向目标域上升，然后在训练持续不变的情况下部分向基线域回退——这一结构性失败被定义为“认知复发”。

Conclusion: 对现实采纳的抵抗不可简化为学习速度，它是一种具有自身独特故障模式的结构性属性。本研究仅作为计算存在性证明，不提供进一步推论。

Abstract: Under the free energy principle, a predictive system does not observe reality directly; it maintains a generative model of the world and experiences that model's best current hypothesis. Can a synthetic environment be made consistent enough that a predictive system's own inference machinery adopts it as this default hypothesis, permanently displacing the environment that first shaped it? We call this state ontological inversion. Because inducing and monitoring such a transition in a nervous system is neither ethical nor technically feasible, we study the underlying computational problem through a controlled proxy: a convolutional variational autoencoder paired with a recurrent latent predictor, whose evidence lower bound objective is mathematically identical, up to sign, to variational free energy itself. The network is trained first on a baseline visual domain, then on a mixed stream in which a swept rehearsal ratio r controls how much baseline content persists during transition to a target domain. Representational capacity, what the latent space can discriminate, is tracked separately from default behavior, what the system generates when left unconstrained. Across a full sweep of 90 runs, the two diverge sharply: representational accuracy stays near ceiling, 0.97 to 0.998, regardless of r, while default behavior spans nearly the system's entire range depending on r alone, a decoupling of learning from acceptance. More strikingly, at intermediate r the system's default output rises toward the target domain, then partially reverts toward the baseline while training continues unchanged, a structural failure we term cognitive relapse. Resistance to reality-adoption is not reducible to learning speed; it is a structural property with its own distinct failure modes, established here as a computational existence proof and nothing further.

</details>


### [104] [Evaluating Reliability in Machine Learning Models for Early Chronic Kidney Disease Prediction: A Systematic Review of Data Leakage and Predictor Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11963)
*Mashrul Hossain,Nafesa Kibria,Fahim Shahriar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对慢性肾脏病（CKD）可解释机器学习预测研究进行了系统性文献综述，提出了一种结构化信息泄漏分类法和定量泄漏评分框架。研究发现，泄漏与性能虚高密切相关：高泄漏研究平均准确率95.48%，而无泄漏研究仅为80.2%，差值约15.28%；且超过80%的预测指标缺乏可复现性。许多报告的性能提升源于方法论缺陷而非真实预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CKD早期检测的机器学习研究虽有显著进展，但存在不一致性和潜在误导性，主要问题是缺乏对方法论可靠性（尤其是数据泄漏、临床指标不一致等）的系统评估。

Method: 通过系统性检索主要学术数据库筛选出19篇相关研究；引入结构化信息泄漏分类法和定量泄漏评分框架；进行跨研究特征稳定性分析。

Result: 高泄漏研究平均准确率95.48%，无泄漏研究80.2%，差异约15.28%；超过80%的预测指标缺乏可靠性和可复现性。

Conclusion: 许多CKD预测研究中报告的性能提升源于数据泄漏等方法论限制，而非模型真实的预测能力。

Abstract: The early detection of Chronic Kidney Disease using machine learning has attracted significant interest in healthcare-related computer science. Despite rapid advancements in this field, many reported studies remain inconsistent and potentially misleading. A significant drawback is the lack of organized evaluation regarding methodological concerns. Key issues include data leakage, limited access to temporal patient records and inconsistency in reported clinical indicators. This research offers a systematic literature review of existing CKD prediction studies using interpretable machine learning techniques, where nineteen relevant studies were selected via systematic searches across major academic databases. To assess methodological reliability, this study introduces a structured taxonomy of information leakage and a quantitative leakage scoring framework to systematically evaluate reliability across CKD prediction studies. The analysis reveals a strong relationship between leakage and inflated performance. Here, High leakage-studies report an average accuracy of 95.48%, compared to 80.2% for leakage-free studies, reflecting an increase of approximately 15.28%. Furthermore, a cross-study feature stability analysis shows that only a small subset of predictors is consistently reproducible, with over 80% lacking reliability. Overall, the findings suggest that many reported performance improvements stem from methodological limitations rather than true predictive capability.

</details>


### [105] [LIDAR-AD: A Decoder-Free Latent-Interaction Dreamer with Action-Residual Chains for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11964)
*Yongzhi Liu,Yang Xiao,Zhong Cao,Zeng Kang,Sunan Zhang,Zhaozhi Dong,Guojun Yu,Weichao Zhuang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LIDAR-AD是一种用于自动驾驶的无解码器潜交互梦境模型，通过冗余缩减的潜对齐替代观测重建，结合残差动作链与对比学习，实现风险感知的长期闭环决策。在仿真和真实场景中均优于现有世界模型方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多源观测包含与控制无关的冗余信息，而可靠驾驶决策依赖风险相关关系、未来动力学和连续动作调整。观测重建和绝对动作建模在学习决策相关的潜动力学方面存在次优问题。

Method: 1) 无解码器架构：用冗余缩减的潜对齐替代观测重建；2) 残差动作链：将车辆控制建模为残差动作更新；3) 残差动作序列对比学习：对齐多步残差驱动 rollout 与未来潜状态；4) 潜-tanh残差参数化保证内部动作可达性。

Result: 在多种模拟驾驶场景中，LIDAR-AD持续优于世界模型基线，获得最高奖励和最佳成功率。在nuPlan导出的日志重构场景中验证了在真实交通布局下的迁移能力。

Conclusion: 无解码器的潜对齐、残差动作建模和对比学习能有效提升自动驾驶中风险感知的状态抽象、连续控制建模和长期动力学预测能力。

Abstract: Autonomous driving requires long-horizon closedloop decision making in dynamic traffic environments. Latent world models offer an effective framework for this problem by enabling imagination-based decision making in compact latent spaces. However, multi-source observations contain controlirrelevant redundancy, whereas reliable driving decisions rely on risk-relevant relations, future dynamics, and continuous action adjustments. This mismatch makes observation reconstruction and absolute action modeling suboptimal for learning decisionrelevant latent dynamics. We propose LIDAR-AD, a decoderfree Latent-Interaction Dreamer with Action-Residual Chains for autonomous driving. LIDAR-AD replaces observation reconstruction with redundancy-reduced latent alignment, encouraging compact representations of risk-relevant relations in multi-source driving inputs. It further models vehicle control as residual action updates and uses residual-action sequence contrastive learning to align multi-step residual-driven rollouts with future latent states. A deterministic analysis shows that the latent-tanh residual parameterization preserves interior action reachability while representing smooth long-horizon control as compact local updates. Together, these designs improve risk-aware state abstraction, continuous-control modeling, and long-horizon dynamics prediction. Extensive experiments across diverse simulated driving scenarios demonstrate that LIDAR-AD consistently outperforms world-model baselines, achieving the highest reward and the best success rate among learning-based methods. Evaluations on nuPlan-derived log-reconstructed scenarios further demonstrate the transferability of LIDAR-AD under real-world traffic layouts.

</details>


### [106] [Beyond Coordinate Gauge: An Audited Protocol for Detecting Donor-Specific Functional Fingerprints after Neural Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11967)
*Truong Xuan Khanh,Phan Thanh Duc*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用仿射校正对齐技术，在神经坍缩收敛后的独立训练网络中成功检测到供体特有的功能指纹，但验证仅限于可检测性，未涉及可移植性或因果持久性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 独立训练的网络缺乏共享的神经元索引参考系，而神经坍缩现象使网络收敛到相似的几何结构，因此需要区分轨迹特定的功能变异在收敛后是否仍然可区分。

Method: 使用五个在MNIST上达到神经坍缩的独立训练网络，通过经过验证的仿射校正对齐方法将供体头部映射到受体坐标空间，并结合泄漏审计进行严格检验。

Result: 所有20个有序供体-受体对均被正确识别，精确排列检验p=0.0083，且对泄漏审计具有稳健性。

Conclusion: 在受控条件下建立了跨网络变异可检测性，但该发现是否能够泛化到其他设置尚待验证，且可移植性和因果持久性不在当前研究范围内。

Abstract: Independently trained neural networks have no shared neuron-index reference frame, so comparing them requires accounting for coordinate freedom. Neural Collapse sharpens this problem: networks converge toward a shared, low-dimensional geometry, raising the question of whether trajectory-specific functional variation remains distinguishable after convergence. We distinguish three claims - detectability, transplantability, and causal persistence - and address the first. Using five independently trained networks reconstructing Neural Collapse on MNIST, we apply a verified affine-correct alignment mapping donor heads into recipient coordinates. Donor-specific functional fingerprints remain distinguishable after recipient-level baseline correction: all 20 ordered donor-recipient pairs are correctly identified, with an exact permutation p=0.0083, robust to a leakage audit. These findings establish detectability under the test used here, but not transplantability or causal persistence. The study shows how alignment, ambiguity diagnostics, and leakage control combine to test cross-network variation in a controlled setting; whether this generalizes beyond it is open.

</details>


### [107] [Self-Evolving In-Context Learning for Direct Pilot-to-Beamformer Design in MU-MISO Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11970)
*Yubo Zhang,Xiaodong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种增强型上下文学习（ICL）框架，用于多用户MISO系统的导频波束赋形。该框架集成ICL-Transformer主干、导频编解码网络和波束赋形编解码网络，无需重训练即可处理多种信道模型，并通过课程学习、自进化机制和失配感知扩展三项创新提升性能。仿真表明该方法在导频设置下优于WMMSE和现有Transformer方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于导频的波束赋形方法通常依赖梯度更新或针对特定信道模型重新训练，缺乏泛化能力和鲁棒性。为突破这一局限，作者希望构建一个无需重训练即可适应多种信道模型、且能缓解失配问题的统一框架。

Method: 提出增强型ICL框架，包含ICL-Transformer主干、导频EDN和波束赋形EDN。通过构建模型特定上下文数据集实现多模型支持；引入课程学习从监督LMMSE标签训练平滑过渡到无监督和速率最大化；采用自进化机制动态扩展和优化上下文数据集；提出失配感知扩展将多种失配纳入框架，绕过显式信道校准。

Result: 消融实验验证了ICL架构和增强训练策略的有效性。在多种通信环境下，该方法能快速适应已知和未知信道模型而无需梯度参数更新，并能通过智能上下文构建缓解失配问题。在导频设置下，该方法持续优于WMMSE基准和基于Transformer的现有方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的ICL框架为导频波束赋形提供了一种高效、泛化能力强的解决方案，消除了重训练需求，并有效应对了信道失配挑战。

Abstract: We develop an enhanced in-context learning (ICL) framework to improve the performance of pilot-based beamforming in multi-user multiple-input single-output (MU-MISO) systems. The proposed scheme integrates the ICL-Transformer backbone with the pilot encoder-decoder network (EDN) and the beamformer EDN. A crucial feature of our ICL network is that it can handle multiple channel models without retraining, enabled by the construction of model-specific context datasets. To improve convergence and robustness, we introduce three key innovations: (a) a curriculum learning (CL) strategy that smoothly transitions from supervised LMMSE-labeled imitation to unsupervised sum-rate maximization, (b) a self-evolving mechanism that dynamically expands and refines the context datasets for all channel models during CL-based training, and (c) a mismatch-aware extension that incorporates several mismatches into the general ICL framework and bypasses explicit channel calibrations. Ablation studies validate the effectiveness of the in-context architecture and enhanced training strategies. Simulation results over diverse communication environments show that the proposed scheme is able to rapidly adapt to both seen and unseen channel models without gradient-based parameter updates, and can mitigate the mismatch issues via intelligent context constructions.
