Furthermore, our scheme consistently outperforms the existing beamforming schemes under pilot-based settings, including the WMMSE benchmark and the recent Transformer-based methods.

</details>


### [108] [Learning to Discretize: Diffusion-Based Adaptive Mesh with Spectral Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11974)
*Zixuan Shen,Bingchuan Wang,Zhi Wang,Yong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种两阶段扩散框架，将自适应网格离散化学习为物理约束的条件生成问题：第一阶段学习r-自适应位移网格，第二阶段基于网格表示预测PDE演化，实验表明该方法在多种PDE场景中与自适应网格和降阶基线竞争，核心结论是离散化应随问题动态学习而非固定规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统神经PDE代理在固定网格上学习场演化，但网格的选择隐含地决定了建模容量在空间、分辨率和谱带宽上的分配，这一设计选择本身应当是可学习的。

Method: 将自适应离散化建模为物理约束的条件生成问题，提出两阶段扩散框架：Stage 1基于观测动力学学习r-自适应位移网格，Stage 2从网格表示预测解演化；网格生成器通过物理感知代理通道、几何有效性约束和局部谱集中度进行正则化。

Result: 在五种PDE体制下的实验表明，基于扩散的学习离散化方法与自适应网格和降阶基线竞争，在固定或手工分配不足的场景中表现尤为突出。

Conclusion: 不存在通用最优网格规则，离散化应以动态依赖的方式学习——不同的空间和谱结构偏好不同的分配行为，将自适应网格从求解器特定的启发式方法重新定义为生成式表示学习问题。

Abstract: Most neural partial differential equation (PDE) surrogates learn how fields evolve after a grid has already been chosen. However, before any operator is applied, the grid has already determined how modeling capacity is allocated across space, resolution, and spectral bandwidth. We argue that this hidden design choice should itself be learnable, leading to a question different from standard operator learning: can a surrogate learn where resolution should exist before predicting field evolution? We formulate adaptive discretization as a physics-constrained conditional generation problem over valid mesh displacements. The success of diffusion models in PDE field prediction suggests their potential for learning adaptive discretizations under similar structured constraints. This leads to a two-stage diffusion framework: Stage 1 learns an r-adaptive displacement mesh conditioned on the observed dynamics, while Stage 2 predicts the solution evolution from the mesh-informed representation. The mesh generator is regularized by physics-aware proxy channels, geometric validity constraints, and local spectral concentration so that adaptation remains physically interpretable and numerically legal. Across five PDE regimes, the results show that diffusion-based learned discretization is competitive with adaptive-mesh and reduced-order baselines, with particularly strong gains in regimes where fixed or handcrafted allocation is insufficient. The main conclusion is not that there exists a universal optimal mesh rule, but that discretization should be learned in a regime-dependent manner: different spatial and spectral structures favor different allocation behaviors. This reframes adaptive meshing for neural PDE solvers from a solver-specific heuristic into a generative representation-learning problem.

</details>


### [109] [Signal-Guided Optimization for Machine Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11975)
*Xujia Li,Dan Li,Jian Lou,Wenjie Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对当前机器遗忘方法缺乏精准引导信号的问题，提出GSUO框架，通过任务特定的细粒度引导信号来优化遗忘过程，在随机子集和类别遗忘任务中均优于14个基线方法，兼顾了遗忘效果、泛化能力和高效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器遗忘方法采用全局粗粒度的干预策略，缺乏精准的引导信号，由于样本在原始训练中的记忆强度不同，统一策略导致部分样本过度遗忘（损害模型效用）而部分样本遗忘不足（残留信息可被隐私攻击利用）。

Method: 提出GSUO（Guidance-Signal-Aware Unlearning Optimization）框架，设计任务特定的细粒度引导信号来指导遗忘过程，适用于随机子集遗忘和类别遗忘两种任务。

Result: 在14个基线方法的对比中，GSUO在遗忘效果和泛化能力上均表现更优，同时实现了高效率和显著的加速效果。

Conclusion: GSUO验证了其作为可靠机器遗忘方法的有效性，能够平衡遗忘彻底性与模型可用性之间的矛盾。

Abstract: Current machine unlearning methods predominantly rely on global, coarse-grained intervention strategies. They lack precise pilot signals to guide the unlearning process and fail to provide differentiable guidance across different unlearning tasks. Due to the varying memorization strengths of samples during original training, such a uniform strategy leads to two problems: some samples are over-unlearned, which harms model utility; while others are under-unlearned, leaving residual information that can be exploited by privacy attacks. In this paper, we propose GSUO, a guidance-signal-aware unlearning optimization framework that designs task-specific fine-grained guidance signals to steer the unlearning process and is applicable to both random-subset and class-wise forgetting tasks. Extensive experiments demonstrate that GSUO outperforms 14 baselines in terms of both unlearning effectiveness and generalization, while achieving high efficiency and significant speedups, validating its effectiveness for reliable machine unlearning.

</details>


### [110] [LiteTopK: Exploiting the Curse of Dimensionality for a Fused Indexer-TopK Kernel in Long-Context Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11976)
*Ziqi Yin,Jianyang Gao,Peiqi Yin,Jiangneng Li,Gao Cong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LITETOPK利用高维空间中的维度诅咒现象，通过采样估计分数范围并在线分桶，设计了一种高效的融合Indexer-TopK内核，在减少全局内存流量、同步开销和内存占用的同时保持精确Top-k正确性，在GLM 5.2预填充阶段实现1.2倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于GPU的Indexer-TopK内核（如DSA）存在全局内存流量过大、同步开销高、内存开销过高的问题，效率低下。

Method: 利用高维空间中向量距离集中于狭窄范围的特性（维度诅咒），先采样小部分数据估计查询-数据分数范围，再在线将候选结果分桶，维持紧凑的近似阈值，仅回写有希望的候选结果。

Result: 在GLM 5.2实际部署场景中，预填充阶段加速1.2倍，且内存开销更低。

Conclusion: LITETOPK通过巧妙利用高维空间特性设计融合内核，在保证精确Top-k正确性的前提下显著提升了Indexer-TopK操作的效率。

Abstract: Indexer-TopK, the operation to compute the scores and select the top-k candidates, is widely used by sparse attention kernels in large language models and vector retrieval in recommendation systems and vector databases. However, existing GPU-based Indexer-TopK kernels like DeepSeek Sparse Attention (DSA) remain inefficient due to excessive global memory traffic, costly synchronization, and prohibitive memory overhead. In this work, we exploit the curse of dimensionality in high-dimensional spaces, where distances between high-dimensional vectors tend to concentrate within a narrow range, to design LITETOPK, a novel and efficient fused Indexer-TopK kernel. LITETOPK first samples a small subset of data to estimate query-data score ranges, then uses these estimates to partition candidate results into bins online. This organization allows the LITETOPK kernel to maintain a tight approximate threshold, write back only promising candidates, reduce unnecessary I/O, substantially lower memory overhead, and still preserve exact Top-k correctness. Experimental results show that LITETOPK accelerates the prefill stage of GLM 5.2 by 1.2x in real-world deployment scenarios while incurring lower memory overhead.

</details>


### [111] [Gene Expression-Informed Jointly Controlled Generative Modeling for Precision Molecular Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11978)
*Hang Yuan,Chen Li,Wenjun Ma,Tadahiko Murata,Yuncheng Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出JoPMol，一种联合控制精准分子生成模型，整合基因表达谱（生物状态）、文本描述（分子结构）和数值量化（化学性质），在统一框架下实现多条件协同控制的分子生成与优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精准分子设计需要同时控制多个条件（如生物相关性和分子设计策略），现有方法难以在统一框架下协调这些异质条件。

Method: 构建将基因表达谱编码的生物状态、文本表达的分子结构信息和数值量化的化学性质整合到统一建模框架中的联合控制分子生成模型。

Result: JoPMol在多项评估指标上优于现有最先进方法，并在迁移任务和生物学模拟场景中展现出强泛化能力。

Conclusion: JoPMol在联合条件控制下能够有效实现候选分子的协同生成与优化，为精准分子设计提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Precision molecular design aims to discover personalized drug candidates through joint control of multiple conditions, such as biological relevance and molecular design strategies. Biological relevance reflects cellular functional states under disease or perturbation conditions, while molecular design strategies provide complementary guidance in terms of structural intentions and property optimization. In this study, we propose JoPMol, a jointly controlled precision molecular generative model that integrates biological states encoded by gene expression profiles with molecular structure information expressed in text, and chemical properties quantified by numerical values within a unified modeling framework. This formulation enables coordinated generation and optimization of candidate molecules under joint condition control. Experimental results show that JoPMol outperforms state-of-the-art methods across multiple evaluation metrics. Moreover, JoPMol demonstrates strong generalization ability in both transfer tasks and biologically grounded simulation scenarios, validating its effectiveness for precision molecular design. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/hala-yh/JoPMol.

</details>


### [112] [Sparse Inter-Layer Dependencies of Transformer FFN Neurons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11990)
*Johannes Knittel,Hanspeter Pfister*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需训练的方法，通过稀疏的上游神经元和注意力输出集合来解释FFN神经元激活，发现前馈网络虽参数密集但存在稀疏的层间依赖结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer中的FFN块参数和计算量大，但由于残差流导致的叠加效应，其内部结构难以解释。需要理解FFN神经元激活是否可由少量上游神经元和注意力输出来解释。

Method: 提出一种无需训练的归因方法，估计上游神经元和注意力输出对目标神经元激活的相对影响。通过用平均值掩码非关键输入，评估保留稀疏子集时激活的保真度。

Result: 少量上游激活和注意力输出即可高保真保留神经元激活；考虑上游层固有稀疏性后有效稀疏度更高；同时应用各层掩码在中等稀疏度下对模型困惑度影响很小。

Conclusion: FFN虽参数密集，但神经元层面存在稀疏且结构化的层间依赖；该方法为电路级可解释性和高效推理中的稀疏路径识别提供了可扩展工具。

Abstract: Feedforward network (FFN) blocks account for a large fraction of the parameters and computation in Transformer architectures, yet their internal structure remains difficult to interpret due to the additive superposition induced by the residual stream. We examine whether the activation of an FFN neuron can be explained by a sparse set of preceding neuron activations and attention outputs. We introduce a training-free attribution method that estimates the relative influence of upstream neurons and attention outputs on a target neuron's activation. Empirically, across models and layers, we find that small subsets of preceding activations and attention outputs suffice to preserve neuron activations with high fidelity when all remaining inputs are masked with their average values. Effective sparsity is even greater when accounting for the inherent activation sparsity of upstream layers. Moreover, applying the neuron-specific masks in all layers simultaneously, such that the induced deviations propagate through the network, leaves model perplexity largely unchanged at moderate sparsity levels. These results demonstrate that, despite dense parameterization, FFNs exhibit sparse and structured inter-layer dependencies at the neuron level. Our method provides a practical, scalable tool for circuit-level interpretability and identifies candidate sparse pathways with potential implications for efficient inference.

</details>


### [113] [Mitigating The Effect of Class Imbalance in Data with Hierarchical and Dependable Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11994)
*Bipin Chhetri,Deepika Giri,Avishek Kadel,Rabin Kumar Karki,Akbar Siami Namin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种层次感知的RoBERTa框架，通过可学习的父类嵌入显式融合CWE层次结构信息，用于网络安全漏洞分类。实验表明，在高维嵌入空间中进行合成插值会破坏CWE层次结构的父子约束，对深度学习模型有负面作用。该框架在不使用数据增强的情况下，在CWE研究概念数据集上取得了0.76的加权F1分数，优于所有基线模型，尤其在少数类上提升显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CWE漏洞分类面临严重的类别不平衡和层次依赖问题，现有的过采样技术（如SMOTE、ADASYN）在层次化文本分类中的有效性尚不明确，需要探索更合理的解决方案。

Method: 提出Hierarchy-Aware RoBERTa框架，通过引入可学习的父类嵌入来显式建模CWE层次结构中的父子约束关系，保持分类学一致性。框架不使用传统过采样方法，而是通过层次感知表示学习来处理类别不平衡。

Result: 在CWE研究概念数据集上，加权F1-score达0.76；少数类别提升显著，其中Class类别的F1-score从BERT基线的0.40提升至0.60；合成过采样对深度学习模型有持续的性能退化作用。

Conclusion: 层次感知的表示学习是比过采样更合理的原则性替代方案，适用于结构化漏洞分类任务；合成插值方法在高维嵌入空间中会破坏层次约束，不适合层次化文本分类。

Abstract: Classifying cybersecurity vulnerabilities using the Common Weakness Enumeration (CWE) taxonomy is challenging due to extreme class imbalance and strong hierarchical dependencies among weakness categories. Although oversampling techniques such as Synthetic Minority Oversampling Technique (SMOTE) and Adaptive Synthetic Sampling (ADASYN) are widely adopted to mitigate class imbalance, their effectiveness for hierarchical CWE text classification remains largely unexplored. This paper proposes a Hierarchy-Aware RoBERTa framework that explicitly incorporates CWE structural information through learnable parent-class embeddings, preserving taxonomic consistency. Our experiments demonstrate that synthetic interpolation in high-dimensional embedding spaces violates the inherent parent-child constraints of the CWE hierarchy, offering only marginal benefits for classical ML models while consistently degrading deep learning architectures. Evaluated on a CWE Research Concept dataset, the proposed model achieves a weighted F1-score of 0.76 without data augmentation, outperforming all baselines with notable gains on minority classes, including the Class category whose F1-score improved from 0.40 to 0.60 over the BERT baseline. Our results suggest that hierarchy-aware representation learning is a more principled alternative to oversampling for structured vulnerability classification.

</details>


### [114] [Institutional Equity Holdings Prediction Using Node Affinities of Dynamic Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12067)
*Emad Izadifar,Zahed Rahmati*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个基于时序图机器学习的机构持股预测基准任务，将持仓预测建模为离散时间二分图上的节点亲和度预测。NAVIS模型在S&P 500数据集上达到0.9127的NDCG，大幅超越现有动态图表示学习方法，但简单的指数移动平均基线（0.8882）也超过了除NAVIS外的所有动态图模型，揭示了机构投资组合的强平滑性与持续性特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SEC 13F filings提供了大型投资经理投资组合决策的丰富时序记录，但由于披露滞后、报告噪声以及机构行为的强持续性，预测未来配置和建模未来需求仍然极具挑战。现有缺乏针对此任务的标准化基准和评估方法。

Method: 将持仓预测构建为离散时间时序二部图（管理人与证券节点）上的节点亲和度预测任务；提出NAVIS（Node Affinity prediction model using Virtual State）模型；在包含99家管理人和S&P 500指数503只证券、209,351条时序边、覆盖2013-2025年48个季度的采样数据集上进行评估；对比了TGB系列动态图表示学习模型与多种启发式方法。

Result: NAVIS达到最优NDCG 0.9127（有特征）/0.9121（无特征），大幅超越所有动态图表示学习竞争模型和启发式方法。简单指数移动平均基线达到0.8882，超越除NAVIS外的所有动态图模型和除Persistent Forecast（0.8891）外的所有启发式方法。领域特定节点特征仅带来<1.2%的边际提升。

Conclusion: 机构投资组合具有强平滑性和持续性，时序与结构信号已能捕获大部分可预测信息，领域特征增益有限。该工作为持仓预测和组合分配领域的时序图机器学习提供了可复现的基准基础。

Abstract: Institutional equity holdings disclosed in SEC Form 13F filings provide a rich temporal record of portfolio decisions by large investment managers. However, forecasting future allocations and modeling future demand remains challenging due to disclosure lags, reporting noise, and strong persistence in institutional behavior. We introduce the first benchmark for these tasks using temporal graph machine learning, framing holdings prediction as node affinity prediction -- i.e., forecasting portfolio weights -- on a discrete-time temporal bipartite graph of managers and securities extracted from preprocessed filings. On a sampled dataset comprising 99 managers and the S\&P 500 index (503 securities, 209,351 temporal edges across 48 quarters from 2013--2025), Node Affinity prediction model using Virtual State (NAVIS) achieves a state-of-the-art test Normalized Discounted Cumulative Gain (NDCG) of 0.9127 with features (0.9121 without), outperforming all dynamic graph representation learning competitors by a substantial margin, and outperforming all heuristic methods. Remarkably, a simple Exponential Moving Average baseline achieves 0.8882, surpassing all dynamic graph models except NAVIS and all heuristics except Persistent Forecast (0.8891), highlighting the strong smoothness and persistence of institutional portfolios. Domain-specific node features provide only marginal gains (<1.2\%), indicating that temporal and structural signals in the 13F ownership graph already capture most of the predictable information. By benchmarking a suite of Temporal Graph Benchmark (TGB) models under the node affinity prediction setting, both with and without features, on real-world 13F data, this work provides a reproducible foundation for temporal graph machine learning in holdings prediction and portfolio allocation.

</details>


### [115] [Sparse Autoencoders for Interpretable Out-of-Distribution Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12094)
*Ayush Karmacharya,Luke Luschwitz,Lucia Romero,Yanan Niu,Joseph Campbell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用稀疏自编码器（SAE）从中间层激活中学习可解释特征，并基于稀疏特征的余弦相似度构建OOD检测分数，在标准基准上取得SOTA性能，同时提供分布偏移的可解释性洞察。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OOD检测方法大多关注最终输出层，忽略了中间网络层中丰富的层次化信息，且缺乏可解释性。

Method: 利用稀疏自编码器（SAE）从中间层激活中学习可解释稀疏特征；提出基于测试样本稀疏特征激活与ID类平均激活之间余弦相似度的OOD分数。

Result: 在标准OOD检测基准上取得了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 该方法不仅性能优异，还能提供关于分布偏移如何影响学习表示的可解释性洞察，是一种有效的后验检测方法。

Abstract: Reliable detection of out-of-distribution (OOD) samples is crucial for the safe deployment of machine learning models. Neural networks often produce overconfident predictions for inputs that deviate from their training data, leading to significant degradation in performance. While many OOD detection methods focus on the final output layer, they neglect the rich hierarchical information present in intermediate network layers. This paper introduces a novel approach that leverages sparse autoencoders (SAEs) to learn interpretable features from these intermediate activations. We find that in-distribution (ID) and OOD data activate distinct sets of these sparse features. We propose a new OOD score derived from the cosine similarity between the sparse feature activations of a test sample and the mean activations of ID classes. Our post-hoc detection method not only achieves state-of-the-art performance on standard OOD detection benchmarks, but yields interpretable insights into how distribution shift affects learned representations.

</details>


### [116] [PFAdapter: Hierarchical LoRA Decomposition for Personalized Federated MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12111)
*Jing Liu,Kun Yang,Yan Wang,Dingkang Yang,Xiaoshuai Hao,Wei Zhang,Yang Liu,Wei Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PFAdapter 是一个面向多模态大模型联邦微调的通信高效框架，通过层级LoRA分解将适配器参数显式分离为全局共享和局部私有组件，结合正交正则化和选择性聚合策略，在VQA-RAD、SLAKE等数据集上相比基线方法准确率提升2.4%-4.8%，通信开销降低近50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦微调协议统一聚合所有参数，混淆了域不变特征与客户端特异性特征，导致个性化不足且通信开销过大，亟需一种能够平衡全局知识聚合与本地适配的高效联邦微调方法。

Method: 提出PFAdapter框架：(1) 层级LoRA分解，将Query和Key投影作为全局共享组件用于捕捉通用多模态语义，Value和Output投影作为局部私有组件用于边缘特定适配；(2) 基于Frobenius范数的正交正则化强制分离全局与局部组件；(3) 选择性聚合协议仅同步全局共享组件，减少近50%通信成本。

Result: 在VQA-RAD、SLAKE、Hateful Memes、CrisisMMD四个数据集上，PFAdapter一致优于现有最先进基线方法，准确率提升2.4%-4.8%。

Conclusion: PFAdapter为资源受限通信网络中智能体AI的部署建立了高效解决方案，有效平衡了全局知识聚合与本地个性化适配。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems are reshaping communications and networking by deploying autonomous intelligent agents capable of collaborative learning while maintaining data privacy at network edges. Within distributed network environments, Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) serve as cognitive engines for edge devices, yet federated fine-tuning faces substantial challenges in balancing global knowledge aggregation with local adaptation under heterogeneous network conditions. Conventional federated protocols typically rely on uniform parameter aggregation, which conflates domain-invariant features with client-specific nuances, thereby resulting in suboptimal personalization and excessive communication overhead. To address these challenges, we propose PFAdapter, a communication-efficient framework introducing hierarchical LoRA decomposition to explicitly separate adapter parameters into global-shared and local-private components. Query and key projections are assigned to global synchronization for capturing universal multimodal semantics across the network, while value and output projections remain localized for edge-specific adaptation. Additionally, orthogonality regularization based on the Frobenius norm enforces strict separation between these components, preventing redundant feature learning. Selective aggregation protocols synchronize only global-shared components across the federated network, preserving local expertise and reducing communication costs by nearly 50%. Extensive experiments on VQA-RAD, SLAKE, Hateful Memes, and CrisisMMD datasets demonstrate that PFAdapter consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, achieving accuracy improvements ranging from 2.4% to 4.8% across diverse edge intelligence tasks. Consequently, our framework establishes an efficient solution for agentic AI deployment in resource-constrained communication networks.

</details>


### [117] [Continual Learning with Elastic Regularization and Synthetic Replay for Federated MLLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12112)
*Jing Liu,Chenxuanyin Zou,Jiayang Ren,Gaoyun Fang,Chengfang Li,Yan Wang,Zhenchao Ma,Bo Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedCMM框架通过三级协同机制（参数级模态感知弹性权重 consolidation、数据级本地生成式回放、聚合级任务相似度梯度聚合）解决联邦多模态大模型持续微调中的灾难性遗忘问题，在视觉-语言任务上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦多模态大模型在动态环境中顺序任务更新会导致灾难性遗忘，即跨视觉、语言和跨模态表示的先验知识被覆盖，阻碍其在安全敏感场景（如内容审核）中的鲁棒部署。

Method: 提出FedCMM框架，包含三级保护：1) 参数级：分别为视觉编码器、语言骨干和跨模态投影器计算独立的Fisher信息矩阵，实现模态感知的弹性权重 consolidation；2) 数据级：每个客户端训练轻量级本地生成式回放模块，生成无原始数据共享的嵌入级多模态回放元组；3) 聚合级：基于梯度余弦相似度的任务感知梯度聚合，自动过滤和重加权客户端更新，抑制冲突方向。

Result: 在两个基准数据集上的实验表明，FedCMM在准确率和向后迁移指标上一致优于最新基线方法。

Conclusion: 全方位的模态感知优化使联邦多模态大模型能够在异构网络AI部署中实现鲁棒的演化式自适应，有效缓解灾难性遗忘问题。

Abstract: Federated fine-tuning of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) across distributed networks enables privacy-sensitive adaptation to evolving data streams, yet a fundamental obstacle prevents robust deployment in dynamic environments: catastrophic forgetting, wherein sequential task updates erase previously acquired knowledge across visual, linguistic, and cross-modal representations. Addressing this challenge is especially critical for autonomous networked AI operating in safety-sensitive domains, such as content moderation, where reliable retention of prior knowledge underpins system integrity. To overcome this, we propose Federated Continual Multimodal Learning (FedCMM), a framework that embeds continual-learning safeguards into the federated optimization loop at three complementary levels. At the parameter level, modality-aware elastic weight consolidation computes separate Fisher information matrices for the vision encoder, language backbone, and cross-modal projector, providing granular, asymmetry-aware protection against modality-specific forgetting. At the data level, each client trains a lightweight local generative replay module to synthesize raw-data-free embedding-level multimodal replay tuples without any raw data sharing. At the aggregation level, Task-similarity-aware gradient aggregation autonomously filters and reweights client updates by gradient cosine similarity, suppressing conflicting directions and stabilizing the global learning trajectory. Extensive experiments on two benchmarks demonstrate that FedCMM consistently outperforms recent baselines on accuracy and backward transfer, confirming that holistic, modality-aware optimization enables robust evolutive adaptation across heterogeneous networked AI deployments.

</details>


### [118] [An Agentic AI Scientific Community for Automated Neural Operator Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12122)
*Luis Loo,Ulisses Braga-Neto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于AI科学社区的自主神经营运子发现方法，通过虚拟实验室群落在引文经济机制下交互，利用LLM驱动的规划者、数值工作者和审稿者三智能体架构，在五个PDE问题上发现高精度、低参数的神经营运子架构。实验表明该社区能持续生成多家族混合架构（99.8%），而消融实验显示移除LLM代理会导致多样性丧失，揭示了神经营运子的"没有免费午餐"定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经营运子架构设计依赖人工专家经验和试错，缺乏自动化、可扩展的发现机制。作者希望构建一个自组织的AI科学社区，通过模拟学术生态中的竞争与合作机制，自动探索和发现高性能的神经营运子架构。

Method: 构建一个由虚拟实验室群组成的AI科学社区，采用引文经济机制：高被引实验室可创建新实验室并淘汰低效实验室。每个实验室包含三个智能体：LLM规划者（提出架构）、数值工作者（训练评估）、LLM审稿者（同行评审）。共享词汇表包括DeepONet、傅里叶、Transformer、小波、残差卷积等算子模块。在五个PDE问题上进行三重复实验验证。

Result: 社区能发现高精度、低参数量的神经营运子架构；99.8%的LLM规划决策选择混合架构；消融实验用规则替代LLM后，社区在多个案例中退化为单一家族堆叠，证明LLM代理对维持多样性不可或缺。

Conclusion: 结果支持神经营运子的"没有免费午餐"定理——不存在通用最优架构；混合多家族架构在不同问题上表现更优；LLM驱动的智能体协同是维持架构多样性和发现高性能架构的关键。

Abstract: We present an agentic approach to autonomous neural operator discovery based on an AI scientific community, which consists of a swarm of virtual laboratories that interact under a citation-based economy of influence. Highly-cited labs found new labs that follow their research direction and replace non-performing labs. Each virtual lab contains three agents: an LLM planner that proposes an architecture, a numerical worker that trains and measures it, and an LLM reviewer that participates in cross-lab peer review. All labs share a common vocabulary consisting of DeepONet (branch-trunk), Fourier, Transformer (attention), wavelet, and residual convolutional neural operator building blocks. We evaluate the neural operator AI scientific community on five problems, namely piecewise regression, the linear advection and Burgers 1D PDEs, and the Navier-Stokes and Darcy flow 2D PDEs, while repeating the simulation three times for each problem. The results show that the neural operator AI scientific community is capable of discovering high-accuracy, low-parameter-count neural operator architectures. All 9,623 LLM calls are logged and audited, which reveals that the virtual lab LLM planners choose to hybridize in 99.8% of their logged decisions, consistently returning multi-family hybrids. Moreover, we conducted an ablation study by replacing the LLM agents in each lab by rule-based alternatives, which caused the scientific community to collapse to non-hybridized single-family stacks in several cases, showing that LLM agency is needed to preserve diversity. The results suggest a no-free-lunch theorem for neural operators: there is no universal winner. The code, configurations, and the complete LLM transcripts are released at https://github.com/luislootx/AI-SC.

</details>


### [119] [From Geometric Recovery to Causal Validation: A Reproducible Audit of Sparse Autoencoder Features, from Superposition Geometry to Causal Inertness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12166)
*Mohamed Abdessalem Bal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出稀疏自编码器（SAE）评估中广泛使用的余弦相似度指标混淆了两种不同性质（解码器几何对齐与编码器激活行为），并通过因果审计发现大量高余弦匹配的特征实际上是因果惰性的（最高占比77%），进而提出了一种模型无关的因果审计工具sae-causal-audit，将懒惰性分解为结构惰性与竞争惰性、读惰性与写惰性，并揭示了原子碰撞信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SAE评估过度依赖余弦相似度等相关性恢复指标，但这些指标混淆了解码器几何对齐与编码器激活行为，无法区分特征是否具有真正的因果关系，导致对特征可解释性的高估。

Method: 对每个恢复的特征进行消融（ablation）和引导（steering）实验进行因果审计；复现Elhage et al. (2022)的叠加相图，识别高稀疏度下的收敛伪影和极端过完备下的扩散共享机制；复现Gao et al. (2024)的TopK与L1对比，提供L1收缩的直接证据；将方法封装为模型无关的确定性管道sae-causal-audit。

Result: 退化SAE中高达77%、良好训练SAE中9%的通过余弦恢复阈值（>=0.90）的特征是因果惰性的，甚至包括余弦接近1.000的匹配；惰性分解为结构惰性（对跖对几何，存在于良好SAE）和竞争惰性（TopK病态，存在于退化SAE），以及读惰性与写惰性，五组对跖对完全解耦；生产级SAE中复制了约14%惰性率的模式，并发现原子碰撞信号——少量原子重复作为数十个不相关概念的最邻近匹配。

Conclusion: 余弦相似度作为SAE评估指标严重不足，因果审计揭示了大量伪恢复特征；惰性现象具有多种成因和方向，需要更精细的因果评估体系；字节级精确复现不可实现，应作为具有明确作用域的声明栈进行报告；原子碰撞信号是另一亟待关注的问题。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) are the standard for decomposing superposed neural representations into interpretable features, and evaluation relies predominantly on correlational recovery metrics -- cosine similarity between ground-truth directions and decoder atoms. We show this conflates two distinct claims: decoder-geometry alignment and encoder-activation behavior. We reproduce the superposition phase diagram of Elhage et al. (2022), identifying a convergence artifact at high sparsity and an under-described diffuse sharing regime at extreme overcompleteness. We reproduce the TopK-versus-L1 comparison of Gao et al. (2024), with direct evidence of L1 shrinkage. Our central result is causal: subjecting every recovered feature to ablation and steering, we find up to 77% of features passing a recovery bar (cosine >= 0.90) in a degraded SAE -- and 9% in a well-trained one -- are causally inert: the matched atom never fires when the feature is present, including matches at cosine ~1.000. We package the method as sae-causal-audit, a model-agnostic instrument with a deterministic pipeline. Re-auditing refines the finding: inertness decomposes by cause into structural inertness (antipodal-pair geometry, present in good SAEs) and competitive inertness (a TopK pathology of degraded SAEs), and by direction into read- and write-inertness, which five antipodal pairs dissociate completely -- unmonitorable yet steerable through the same atom, with steering specificities of 143-310 attached to zero ablation effects. We document why byte-exact reproducibility is unavailable by construction, and propose reporting it as a stack of claims with explicit scopes. Applying the instrument to a production SAE reproduces the pattern at small scale (14% inert) and surfaces an atom-collision signal: a handful of atoms recur as the nearest match for dozens of unrelated concepts, replicated across three batches.

</details>


### [120] [Forgetful Attention: A Trainable Support-Vector Memory with Certified Selection and Exact Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12204)
*Vishwajith Ramesh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出支持向量注意力（SV-Attention），一种基于单类SVM的在线记忆机制，其支持向量的稀疏性可证明移除token不影响输出，并通过可逆增量求解器实现精确删除与状态恢复。实验表明其在精确删除、遗忘记忆、长尾召回等任务上优于基线，在enwik8上取得8.6%的BPC改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于注意力的在线记忆机制无法保证丢弃某个token后输出保持不变，也无法完全消除其影响力，缺乏可证明的删除证书。需要一种既能高效在线学习又能支持精确删除与遗忘的记忆机制。

Method: 将注意力视为一个带固定盒参数C的单类SVM，记忆权重即为支持系数；利用SVM的主动集划分使保留token（支持向量）权重非零、可遗忘token（非支持向量）权重恰好为零，从而提供输出保持性删除证书；设计可逆增量求解器，通过删除一个token并更新KKT逆矩阵，恢复与在该C下重新训练完全相同的状态。

Result: 在fp64实验中，删除与重新训练得到相同划分（最优解唯一时），决策函数中位数偏差约10^-9（学习键下10^-13）；最坏情况10^-2仅发生在病态重复样本上。训练加速达9,125 token/s（3.22M参数模型），但比MPS softmax参考慢35.8倍。在等预算下，认证选择达到0.86 vs 0.32的稀有项召回，MIMIC-IV流上维持0.80 vs 0.05的退化小时数。enwik8上混合模型得2.178 BPC vs 2.383（8.6%改进，p=0.001）；TinyStories方向为正但不显著（p=0.057）。

Conclusion: SV-Attention提供了一种具有可证明删除证书的注意力记忆机制，支持精确遗忘、编辑、患者记录删除及可遗忘检索记忆，在长尾召回与遗忘性能上显著优于基线，但训练开销较大，仍待进一步优化效率。

Abstract: Attention can be viewed as an online learner over context, yet existing test-time memories cannot certify that dropping a token leaves outputs unchanged or delete its influence outright. We introduce Support Vector Attention (SV-Attention), a max-margin memory whose weights are support coefficients of a one-class SVM with fixed box parameter C. Its active-set partition gives reserve tokens exactly zero weight, certifying output-preserving eviction; a reversible incremental solver deletes a token to recover the state produced by retraining without it under the same C. In fp64 experiments, decrement and refit recover identical partitions whenever the optimum is unique, and their decision functions match to a median deviation of about 10^-9 (10^-13 on learned keys); the 10^-2 worst case is confined to ill-conditioned duplicates and remains below coefficient decay in every regime. The exact path reuses the maintained KKT inverse in a custom backward. Training uses a separate stabilized batched approximation and does not carry the exact-deletion certificate; it reaches 9,125 tokens/s on a 3.22M-parameter model, while remaining 35.8 times slower than an MPS softmax reference. At matched budgets, certified selection reaches 0.86 vs. 0.32 rare-item recall and retains 0.80 vs. 0.05 deterioration hours on real MIMIC-IV streams. We also demonstrate surgical forgetting, exact editing, patient-record deletion, and a forgettable retrieval memory over real sentence embeddings. On enwik8, the hybrid obtains 2.178 BPC vs. 2.383 for a matched-state sliding-window Transformer across seven seeds (8.6% paired improvement, p=0.001); a three-seed TinyStories result is directionally positive but not significant (p=0.057).

</details>


### [121] [Speculate with Memory: Lossless Acceleration for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12236)
*Yu Li,Qinyuan Ye,Prafulla Kumar Choubey,Jiaxin Zhang,Chien-Sheng Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种带有三种在线记忆系统的投机执行方法，通过从过去的智能体轨迹中学习来提升LLM代理的性能，在多个基准测试上实现了19-39%的动作预测准确率提升和最高2.5倍的观测预测性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的投机执行方法是无状态的，在任务之间丢弃所有信息，导致预测质量无法随经验积累而提升。本文旨在通过引入记忆机制来克服这一局限性。

Method: 为投机器配备三种在线记忆系统：对比转换表（跟踪动作序列统计）、情景记忆（检索上下文相似片段）和混淆追踪器（抑制重复错误）。

Result: 在六个基准测试上，动作预测准确率提升19-39%，观测预测任务性能提升高达2.5倍，且增益随着记忆积累持续增长并泛化到不同成本的投机模型。

Conclusion: 记忆增强的投机执行是无损的（在空闲时间运行，不增加墙钟成本），且智能体的轨迹与非投机执行完全相同。

Abstract: Speculative execution accelerates LLM agents by using a smaller, cheaper model to predict and pre-launch the next step while the environment is idle. However, existing speculators are stateless and discard all information between tasks, preventing prediction quality from improving with experience. We equip the speculator with three online memory systems that learn from past agent trajectories: a contrastive transition table tracking action-sequence statistics, an episodic memory retrieving contextually similar segments, and a confusion tracker suppressing recurring errors. We evaluate this approach on six benchmarks spanning three speculation types: action prediction, observation prediction, and chained prediction. Memory-augmented speculation yields a 19--39\% relative accuracy improvement on action prediction and up to a $2.5\times$ increase on observation prediction tasks with repetitive action spaces. These gains grow continuously as memory accumulates and generalize across speculator models of varying cost. All speculation is lossless because it runs during idle time at zero added wall-clock cost, and the actor's trajectory is identical to non-speculative execution.

</details>


### [122] [Proximity Features: Privacy-Compliant Cold-Start Personalization at Airbnb](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12246)
*Wei Jiang,Bin Xu,Hui Gao,Bharathi Thangamani,Weiwei Guo,Sundar Srinivasavaradhan,Tracy Yu,Huiji Gao,Michael Kinoti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对Airbnb这类高决策成本、低频交易的双边市场平台中大量用户缺乏历史行为数据的问题，本文提出了Proximity Features系统——一种基于地理邻近性的隐私合规特征系统，通过Geo-IP数据和自适应聚类算法将用户分组（约1000人/组）聚合生成信号，无需持久化个体标识符，已在生产环境中部署并取得显著的预订提升效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Airbnb等双边市场中大量用户（登出用户、首次访客）缺乏足够历史行为数据，传统用户级特征不可用；隐私法规和第三方Cookie限制进一步加剧了用户标识追踪的困难。

Method: 提出Proximity Features系统：基于Geo-IP数据进行自适应聚类，将地理位置相近的用户聚合为约1000人的组，在组级别上产生聚合用户信号，推理时无需持久化个体标识符；仅使用经用户同意的聚合数据，内置隐私保护。

Result: 已在Airbnb生产环境部署，服务于营销落地页和目的地推荐等多个场景；在线A/B实验显示预订量有统计显著提升，其中历史数据缺失或过时的用户获益最大。

Conclusion: Proximity Features有效解决了低频交易平台中冷启动用户的个性化推荐难题，在保护用户隐私的同时显著提升业务指标，为隐私受限环境下的个性化推荐提供了一种可行的工程方案。

Abstract: Personalization in two-sided marketplaces relies heavily on user-level features, yet for platforms with infrequent, high-consideration purchases, a large fraction of users lack sufficient history for effective recommendation, spanning both paid and organic channels. At Airbnb, a substantial share of search requests comes from logged-out or first-time users, with this challenge especially pronounced on paid-channel landing pages, leaving traditional user-level features unavailable for a large fraction of traffic. Privacy regulations and increasing restrictions on third-party cookies further limit identifier-based tracking for non-essential use cases. This paper introduces Proximity Features, a privacy-compliant feature system that groups users by geographic proximity using geo-IP data and an adaptive clustering algorithm, producing aggregated user-level signals for groups of approximately 1,000 nearby users without requiring a persistent individual identifier at inference time. Privacy is preserved by design: the pipeline operates on consented, aggregated data only within consent-gated privacy controls.
  The system is deployed in production at Airbnb, serving multiple surfaces including marketing landing pages and destination recommendation, with engagement emails integration under way. Online A/B experiments demonstrate statistically significant lifts in bookings, with the largest gains observed among users with absent or stale history.

</details>


### [123] [Understanding Structured Health Data through Interaction-Aware Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12255)
*Ji Hwan Park,Ying Ding,Tianjin Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究探索了交互感知的混合专家模型，利用结构化健康记录的多层级视图进行中风后僵硬预测。尽管性能提升有限，路由归因揭示了不同视图间的系统性重要性差异，强调了视图构建对模型可解释性的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型在处理多源异构的结构化健康记录时，往往缺乏对不同视图间交互的建模能力，且模型可解释性不足。作者希望利用混合专家模型进行交互感知，提升中风后僵硬预测的准确性与可解释性。

Method: 采用交互感知的混合专家（Mixture-of-Experts, MoE）架构，利用结构化健康记录的多层级视图作为输入，通过专家网络的动态路由机制学习不同视图的重要性。

Result: 模型在性能提升方面效果有限（最小性能增益），但路由归因分析揭示了不同视图之间存在系统性的重要性差异。

Conclusion: 虽然混合专家模型在性能上的直接提升不大，但视图构建的质量和设计方式对模型的可解释性具有关键影响。路由归因可作为理解模型决策的重要工具。

Abstract: We study interaction-aware mixture-of-experts for post-stroke rigidity prediction using multi-level views of structured health records. Despite minimal performance gains, routing attribution reveals systematic importance differences across views, underscoring view construction as key to interpretability.

</details>


### [124] [From Many to Meaningful: Feature-Guided Zero-Shot Chronic Kidney Disease Screening Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12260)
*Muhammad Ashad Kabir,Sirajam Munira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种特征引导的零样本框架，利用大语言模型（LLMs）在无需数据集训练的情况下，仅基于少量社区可获取的临床特征进行慢性肾病（CKD）早期筛查。通过ML选择的核心特征子集，在三个国家的异构数据集上，四个LLM（LLaMA-3、Qwen-3、Mistral、GPT-4o-mini）的零样本性能均达到筛查可用水平，且优于全特征集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习CKD筛查方法依赖大规模标注数据、高成本病理检测或高维临床特征，且对人群分布变化鲁棒性差，难以部署于社区和资源有限的筛查场景；而LLM的零样本能力尚未被探索用于CKD早期筛查。

Method: (1) 基于ML分析从全特征集中选择一组紧凑且临床相关的社区可获取特征子集；(2) 将表格患者记录通过标准化提示模板序列化为文本；(3) 在零样本设置下（无数据集特定训练），使用四个LLM（LLaMA-3、Qwen-3、Mistral、GPT-4o-mini）进行推理；(4) 在覆盖三个国家的三个异构CKD数据集上评估全特征集与所选特征子集的性能。

Result: 所选特征子集在所有模型和数据集上相比全特征集均取得了统计显著的一致提升，在平衡准确率和概率估计方面达到适合筛查的性能水平。

Conclusion: LLM能够支持临床有意义、无需训练的CKD筛查，仅需极少的社区可获取患者特征，在真实筛查场景中可作为传统ML方法的一种实用补充。

Abstract: Early screening of chronic kidney disease (CKD) is essential for preventing irreversible progression; however, many machine learning (ML)-based screening methods remain difficult to deploy in community and resource-limited screening settings due to their reliance on large labeled datasets, resource-intensive pathology tests, or high-dimensional clinical features, and limited robustness to population and distributional shifts. This study examines the feasibility of using large language models (LLMs) for early-stage CKD screening in a zero-shot setting, without dataset-specific training. We propose a feature-guided zero-shot framework that evaluates LLM performance using a selected set of clinically meaningful, readily available community-based features, rather than exhaustive clinical inputs. Feature selection was guided by ML-based analysis to identify a compact, clinically relevant subset of variables. Tabular patient records were subsequently serialized into text using standardized prompt templates to enable zero-shot inference. The zero-shot performance of four LLMs (LLaMA-3, Qwen-3, Mistral, and GPT-4o-mini) was evaluated using both the full feature set and the selected subset. Generalizability was assessed across three heterogeneous CKD datasets spanning three countries. Across models and datasets, the selected feature set yielded consistent and statistically significant improvements in balanced accuracy and probability estimates, achieving performance levels suitable for screening purposes. These findings suggest that LLMs can support clinically meaningful, training-free CKD screening using minimal community-accessible patient features, offering a practical complement to conventional ML methods in real-world screening contexts.

</details>


### [125] [Saturation Makes Quantization Error Additive: A Coverage Model with a Certificate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12266)
*Joshua Hill*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过布尔立方体上的集合函数分析，发现混合精度量化中4比特权重的损失主要由逐层效应解释（方差解释率85-93%），提出覆盖率模型（coverage model）作为损失预测器，在30B-355B参数模型上优于其他分配方法，且在低于4比特时仍能保持代码和推理任务的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验混合精度量化中一个常见前提：量化一组层导致的损失是否可以通过单独或成对测量的灵敏度来重构。该前提被HAWQ、CoopQ等基于灵敏度的方法广泛使用，但在实际部署的4比特权重量化精度下尚未得到充分验证。

Method: 将量化层集的损失变化f(S)视为布尔立方体上的集合函数，通过两种经典基变换分析其结构。提出覆盖率模型f(S)=c(1-∏(1-a_i))，以及加性模型（最优一阶预测器）。利用Parseval恒等式计算加性模型的均方误差，作为预测质量的证书。

Result: 1) 在部署分布中，85-93%的f方差由逐层效应解释；2) 加性模型的单调变换对配置排序的错误率不超过2%；3) 覆盖率模型从L个拟合断裂率（break-rates）复现f的方差分布，误差在几个百分点内；4) 在30B-355B参数模型上，等内存分配下KL散度最低；5) 低于4比特时，梯度灵敏度方法不再产生有效生成，而本方法仍能解决代码和推理任务。

Conclusion: 逐层效应是混合精度量化中损失的主要解释因素，加性模型和覆盖率模型均能有效预测配置损失并指导分配，在极低比特（<4比特）下仍具有实用价值，优于传统的基于梯度灵敏度的分配方法。

Abstract: Mixed-precision quantization must decide which parts of a model to keep at higher precision. A common premise, shared by sensitivity-based methods such as HAWQ and CoopQ, is that the loss from quantizing a set of layers can be reconstructed from per-layer or pairwise sensitivities measured in isolation. We test this premise at the 4-bit weight-and-activation precisions now being deployed, treating the change in loss $f(S)$ from quantizing a layer set $S$ as a set function on the Boolean cube and analyzing it through two classical changes of basis. This analysis yields two findings. First, across configurations drawn from the deployment distribution, 85--93\% of the variance of $f$ is explained by per-layer effects alone. Second, a monotone transform of a sum of per-layer terms reproduces $f$'s ranking of configurations, misordering at most 2\% of pairs.
  We propose the coverage model $f(S)=c\bigl(1-\prod_{i\in S}(1-a_i)\bigr)$, which reproduces the measured variance profile of $f$ to within a few percent from its $L$ fitted break-rates. This structure supports two predictors of a configuration's loss, each with $L+1$ parameters. The additive model is the optimal first-order predictor. By Parseval's identity its mean-squared error equals the variance of $f$ left unexplained by per-layer effects, which we measure on full lattices, estimate out of sample at full-network scale, and report with every result as a certificate of how well any additive model can do. The coverage model itself is the second predictor.
  As allocators at matched memory, they attain the lowest KL divergence among the compared allocators on models from 30B to 355B parameters. Below four bits, the resulting allocations continue to solve code and reasoning tasks at budgets where allocations from gradient sensitivities no longer produce terminating generations.

</details>


### [126] [Track, Rank, Crack: Epistemic Working Memory Scales Multi-Hop Reasoning in Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12267)
*Ning Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SLEUTH，通过结构化认知工作记忆（确认事实、活跃假设、开放问题）显式维护推理状态，解决语言代理在长链推理中因上下文稀释导致的性能衰退问题。实验表明，结构化状态是有效承诺的必要条件，且推理组织方式比模型原始能力更关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言代理在长链推理中性能急剧下降，即使每一步都简单。原因是上下文稀释：代理的推理状态仅隐式存在于不断增长的上下文窗口中，早期发现被后续检索淹没。

Method: 提出SLEUTH，通过结构化认知工作记忆显式维护推理状态：确认事实（附来源）、活跃假设（按证据排序）、开放问题（驱动下一步行动）。此外，引入轻量级承诺触发器解决证据充分性问题。

Result: 在5个多跳基准和5个基线方法上，SLEUTH优势随难度增长：HotpotQA上+5分，4跳链上+11分，超越无需多轮迭代的Reflexion。承诺触发器仅在结构化状态下有效，非结构化代理无提升。较弱模型上强制执行协议可提升最高+19分。

Conclusion: 推理组织方式（如何组织推理）比原始模型能力更重要，是扩展多跳推理的关键因素。结构化认知状态是有效承诺的必要条件。

Abstract: Language agents that interleave reasoning and tool use degrade sharply as reasoning chains lengthen, even when each individual step is easy. We trace this to context dilution: an agent's investigative state (what it has confirmed, what it suspects, and what it still needs) lives only implicitly in a growing context window, where early discoveries are buried under later retrievals. We introduce SLEUTH, which makes this state explicit and actionable through a structured epistemic working memory: the agent maintains Confirmed Facts grounded to sources, Active Hypotheses ranked by evidence, and Open Questions that directly drive its next action. Across five multi-hop benchmarks and five established baselines, SLEUTH's advantage grows with difficulty, from +5 points on HotpotQA to +11 on 4-hop chains, surpassing Reflexion without multiple episodes. Analyzing where the remaining gap lies, we identify the evidence sufficiency problem: agents often find the answer but fail to commit, exhausting their budget on needless verification. A lightweight commitment trigger fixes this, but only when the agent already maintains structured state: the identical trigger applied to an unstructured agent yields no improvement, isolating organized epistemic state as the necessary condition for effective commitment. Finally, enforcing protocol adherence on a weaker model recovers up to +19 points on the hardest problems, showing that how an agent organizes its reasoning, not raw model capability, is the active ingredient for scaling multi-hop reasoning.

</details>


### [127] [Quantum Port-Hamiltonian Neural Networks: Learning Conservative and Dissipative Dynamics via Measurement-Induced Nonlinearity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12269)
*Dibakar Sigdel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出量子端口-哈密顿神经网络(Q-pHNNs)，通过同构哈密顿映射(IHM)将经典动力学中的互联矩阵与酉门演化、耗散矩阵与测量诱导非线性对应，构建保结构学习的参数化量子电路系列，在非线性摆和阻尼谐振子实验上验证了能量守恒与无源性约束的天然满足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统神经网络学习经典动力学时，往往通过惩罚项近似物理约束(如能量守恒、无源性)，缺乏结构保证。本文旨在利用量子计算的天然特性(酉演化保能量、测量引入耗散)，设计一种保结构量子神经网络，使物理约束由架构本身而非损失函数强制实现。

Method: 提出同构哈密顿映射(IHM)：将哈密顿系统的斜对称互联矩阵J映射为酉门演化(保能量)，正半定耗散矩阵R映射为基于中电路测量和经典前馈的测量诱导非线性(MINL)。构建四种架构：(1)量子HNN，通过参数位移规则精确提取哈密顿方程；(2)基于玻恩规则测量的Q-pHNN实现耗散；(3)联合学习能量ansatz和阻尼系数的Q-pHNN；(4)拓扑纠缠的量子图神经网络用于N节点耦合相量网络。

Result: 在非线性摆和阻尼谐振子实验上：(i)使用辛积分器和尺度修正后相对能量漂移仅1.35%；(ii)MINL电路实现100%能量单调性；(iii)在无阻尼系数直接监督下，从向量场快照中识别阻尼系数的误差为12.1%。

Conclusion: Q-pHNN框架成功将经典端口-哈密顿系统的结构约束(守恒性、无源性)编码到量子电路设计中，验证了量子计算在保结构动力学学习中的可行性，为量子物理信息学习提供了新范式。

Abstract: We introduce Quantum Port-Hamiltonian Neural Networks (Q-pHNNs), a family of parameterised quantum circuits that learn classical dynamics in a structure-preserving manner. The framework relies on the Isomorphic Hamiltonian Mapping (IHM): the skew-symmetric interconnection matrix $\mathbf{J}$ corresponds to unitary gate evolution, and the positive-semidefinite dissipation matrix $\mathbf{R}$ corresponds to Measurement-Induced NonLinearity (MINL) realised via mid-circuit measurement and classical feedforward. This ensures conservation and passivity are enforced by construction rather than penalty terms. We instantiate the IHM in four architectures: (1) a Quantum HNN that learns conservative energy manifolds and extracts Hamilton's equations exactly via the Parameter-Shift Rule; (2) a Q-pHNN using Born-rule measurement for dissipation; (3) a Q-pHNN jointly learning the energy ansatz and damping coefficient; and (4) a topology-entangled Quantum Graph Neural Network for $N$-node coupled-phasor networks. Experiments on the nonlinear pendulum and damped harmonic oscillator demonstrate: (i)~$1.35\%$ relative energy drift with a symplectic integrator and scale correction; (ii)~$100\%$ energy monotonicity for the MINL circuit; and (iii)~$12.1\%$ error in damping-coefficient identification from vector-field snapshots with no direct supervision on the damping coefficient.

</details>


### [128] [A hybrid analytical-PINN model for subsurface simulation of geothermal heat exchangers in heterogeneous underground](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12271)
*Moke Rao,Thomas Hamacher,Smajil Halilovic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种参数化物理信息神经网络，用于求解带有钻孔换热器（BHEs）作为奇异源的非均匀土壤热问题。通过解析线源模型去除奇异性，利用梯度热导率的显式公式实现物理信息学习，并利用叠加原理将学习到的校正量作为通用校正器。该方法通过温度分解将非均匀响应近似转化为校正项，有效训练神经网络，并通过数值测试验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决带有钻孔换热器作为奇异源的非均匀土壤热问题中，传统数值方法难以处理奇异性及土壤非均匀性的挑战。

Method: (i) 利用解析线源模型自然去除奇异性；(ii) 采用梯度热导率显式公式进行物理信息参数化学习；(iii) 通过叠加原理将学习到的校正量作为通用校正器。将温度变化分解，将非均匀响应的近似转化为校正扩散/平流-扩散方程，使用物理信息神经网络逼近单钻孔单位取热率下的通用校正器，损失函数包含物理信息项和数据锚定项，并引入位置指示函数作为网络特征输入。

Result: 基于三种不同解析模型的数值测试，验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的参数化物理信息神经网络框架能有效处理奇异源和非均匀土壤热问题，校正器具有通用性，为实际工程应用提供了高效求解方案。

Abstract: In this paper, a parametric physics-informed neural network for solving the heterogeneous soil thermal problem with borehole heat exchangers (BHEs) as singular sources is developed. There are three novel features in the present framework; namely, (i) the singularity is naturally removed by using analytical line source models; (ii) using the explicit formulation for gradient thermal conductivity enables physics-informed learning of the parametrization featuring the conductivity; (iii) the learned correction is utilized as an efficient universal corrector via superposition principles. We first introduce the decomposition of the temperature change and transform the approximation of the entire heterogeneous response to the correction compensating the difference between the practical solution and idealized homogeneous approximation. In such a way, the delta function singularity is excluded and the bulk heat transfer is captured for the sake of facilitating the effective training of the neural network. The original problem is then reformulated as a governing correction diffusion or advection-diffusion equation subject to a homogeneous initial condition. The linearly varying thermal conductivity is used to model the soil heterogeneity. We propose a physics-informed neural network to approximate a universal corrector with respect to a single borehole with unit heat extraction rate. As a result, the network is trained by minimizing the physics-informed and data-anchored loss function that is evaluated for sampled conductivity parameters on adaptively selected training points. In addition, we include the location indicator function regarding the source as a feature input of network and find that it helps the network to process the local information. We perform numerical tests to exhibit the effectiveness of the proposed method based on three different analytical models.

</details>


### [129] [Gradient Flow Dynamics and Implicit Bias of Diagonal Linear Networks under Infinitesimal Initialization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12332)
*Jiajie Zhao,Jianxing Wang,Junjie Yang,Zhiwei Bai,Yaoyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了无限小初始化下对角线性网络在回归任务中的梯度流动力学，将Pesme & Flammarion (2023)的理论推广到深层对角线性网络及更广泛的两层对角线性网络，证明训练轨迹可由提出算法等价刻画，并收敛到修正的l1范数最小化解，从而建立无限小初始化下两种架构的隐式偏差对应修正l1范数，同时识别出结构不变流形(SIM)作为塑造学习过程的关键几何结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Pesme & Flammarion (2023)关于两层对角线性网络无限小初始化下隐式偏差的理论推广到更深层网络及更广义的两层网络架构，并揭示其动力学背后的几何机制。

Method: 理论分析：扩展定理1，提出算法1等价刻画训练轨迹，证明算法收敛到修正l1范数最小化问题，识别结构不变流形(SIM)作为关键几何结构。

Result: 1) 训练轨迹可由算法1等价表征；2) 算法收敛到修正l1范数最小化解；3) 两种网络架构在无限小初始化下的隐式偏差均为修正l1范数；4) SIM是塑造学习过程的关键几何结构。

Conclusion: 无限小初始化下，深层及广义两层对角线性网络的隐式偏差对应于修正l1范数最小化，其动力学受结构不变流形(SIM)的几何结构调控。

Abstract: We study the gradient flow dynamics of diagonal linear networks for regression tasks under infinitesimal initialization. Extending Theorem 1 from Pesme & Flammarion (2023), we generalize the analysis to both deep diagonal linear networks and a broader class of two-layer diagonal linear networks (as defined in Definition 4.1). Specifically, we demonstrate that the training trajectories of these models can be equivalently characterized by the proposed Algorithm 1. We further prove that this algorithm converges to the solution of a modified $ \mathcal{l}_1 $ norm minimization problem. As a result, we establish that the implicit bias of both network architectures corresponds to a modified $ \mathcal{l}_1 $ norm in the regime of infinitesimal initialization. Additionally, we provide insights into the underlying mechanisms governing these dynamics by identifying the Structural Invariant Manifold (SIM) (Zhao et al., 2026) as the key geometric structure that shapes the learning process.

</details>


### [130] [Generating Developable 3D Molecules via Pocket-Conditioned Diffusion and Property-Aware Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12349)
*Ruoxi Gao,Jiangweizhi Peng,Ziqi Chen,Frazier N. Baker,David C. Kombo,John L. Kane,Andrew A. Scholte,Yi Li,Matthew J. LaMarche,Luigi I. Iconaru,Hans-Peter Biemann,Mingyi Hong,Xia Ning*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出conDitar-dev，一种基于条件扩散模型的結構导向药物设计框架，通过多尺度口袋表示学习、口袋条件扩散模型和生成时优化模块，生成具有强结合亲和力和优良ADMET性质的配体分子。在人类疾病靶点基准上，结合评分达-8.85 kcal/mol，ADMET性能提升高达73%。通过PD-L1和CSF1R靶点的实验验证，获得了KD值3.49-3.75 μM的PD-L1配体及IC50低至200 nM的选择性CSF1R抑制剂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的SBDD方法存在三个主要问题：口袋和配体表示学习耦合、仅在原子层面建模相互作用、以及优先考虑结合亲和力而忽视其他可开发性性质。因此需要一种能够解耦表示学习、多尺度建模相互作用并同时优化结合亲和力和ADMET特性的新框架。

Method: 提出conDitar-dev框架，包含三个模块：(1) msPRL：预训练的多尺度口袋表示学习模块；(2) conDitar：受msPRL表示引导的口袋条件扩散模型；(3) paOPT：生成时优化配体可开发性的方法。整体框架实现了口袋-配体表示的联合学习、原子层级以上的多尺度相互作用建模，以及结合亲和力与ADMET性质的联合优化。

Result: 在人类疾病靶点基准上，结合评分达-8.85 kcal/mol，优于现有SBDD基线。ADMET性能提升高达73%。PD-L1靶点：两个直接生成分子的SPR KD值分别为3.49 μM和3.75 μM。CSF1R靶点：基于设计分子的hit expansion获得IC50低至200 nM的选择性抑制剂，并发现药物重定位机会。

Conclusion: conDitar-dev成功解决了现有扩散SBDD方法的三大局限，通过实验验证了其生成具有强结合亲和力和优良ADMET特性的可开发分子的能力，并在两个治疗靶点上获得了实验验证的活性分子，展示了在药物发现中的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Drug discovery and development is time-consuming and resource-intensive, motivating computational approaches such as diffusion models for de novo drug design. Many such models follow the structure-based drug design (SBDD) paradigm, generating molecules to fit a target binding pocket. However, existing diffusion-based SBDD methods typically couple pocket and ligand representation learning, model interactions only at the atom level, and prioritize binding affinity over other developability properties. Here, we introduce conDitar-dev, a conditional diffusion-based SBDD framework for generating ligands with strong binding affinities and favorable ADMET properties. It consists of three modules: msPRL, a pretrained multi-scale pocket representation learning module; conDitar, a pocket-conditioned diffusion model guided by msPRL representations; and paOPT, a generation-time method for optimizing ligand developability. On a newly curated benchmark of human disease targets, conDitar outperforms state-of-the-art SBDD baselines, achieving an average binding score of -8.85 kcal/mol. Across five ADMET properties, conDitar-dev improves performance by up to 73% over conDitar. To further validate the abilities of conDitar-dev to generate developable molecules, we have applied it to two validated druggable targets: programmed death-ligand 1 (PD-L1) and colony-stimulating factor 1 receptor (CSF1R) proteins. Top-ranked generatively designed molecules and their analogs have been experimentally synthesized and biologically tested. Two molecules generated directly by conDitar-dev for PD-L1 exhibited SPR-derived $K_D$ values of 3.49 and 3.75 $μ$M, respectively. Hit expansion based on conDitar-dev-designed molecules identified selective CSF1R inhibitors with IC$_{50}$ values as low as 200 nM, while also uncovering opportunities for drug repositioning.

</details>


### [131] [Reducing information dependency does not cause training data privacy. Adversarially non-robust features do](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12354)
*Rasmus Torp,Shailen K. Smith,Adam Breuer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文挑战了“信息依赖性（包括死记硬背）驱动训练数据暴露于图像重建攻击”的主流观点，证明广泛的数据暴露可以在没有死记硬背的情况下发生，而是由与对抗鲁棒性相关的可调连接引起。通过三个令人惊讶的实验结果和提出的AT-AT训练方法，揭示了隐私泄露与对抗鲁棒性之间的新权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战现有认知：信息依赖性（如死记硬背）是导致训练数据在图像重建攻击中被泄露的主要原因。作者通过三个反直觉的实验结果指出，即使没有死记硬背，模型仍然可能严重泄露训练数据，且现有防御机制未真正解决信息依赖性。

Method: 通过三个实验揭示反直觉现象：（1）MIAs防御不减少HSIC信息依赖度量；（2）最大记忆化模型仍能抵抗MIA重建；（3）缺失97%像素训练的模型仍可被MIA重建。引入抗对抗训练（AT-AT），有意学习非鲁棒特征以获得更好的隐私保护和更高精度。

Result: 发现MIA下的隐私泄露源自对抗样本文献中的“非鲁棒”特征（可泛化但不可感知且不稳定的特征）。现有MIA防御通过无意中使模型偏向此类特征来获得隐私改进。AT-AT方法在防御重建攻击和精度上均优于最先进防御方法。

Conclusion: 修订了关于训练数据暴露的主流理解，揭示了一个新的隐私-鲁棒性权衡：对抗鲁棒性与隐私泄露之间存在可调的联系，非鲁棒特征在MIA隐私保护中起因果作用。

Abstract: In this paper, we challenge the prevailing view that information dependency (including rote memorization) drives training data exposure to image reconstruction attacks. We show that extensive exposure can persist without rote memorization and is instead caused by a tunable connection to adversarial robustness. We begin by presenting three surprising results: (1) recent defenses that inhibit reconstruction by Model Inversion Attacks (MIAs), which evaluate leakage under an idealized attacker, do not reduce standard measures of information dependency (HSIC); (2) models that maximally memorize their training datasets remain robust to MIA reconstruction; and (3) models trained without seeing 97% of the training pixels, where recent information-theoretic bounds give arbitrarily strong privacy guarantees under standard assumptions, can still be devastatingly reconstructed by MIA.
  To explain these findings, we provide causal evidence that privacy under MIA arises from what the adversarial examples literature calls ``non-robust'' features (generalizable but imperceptible and unstable features). We further show that recent MIA defenses obtain their privacy improvements by unintentionally shifting models toward such features. To establish this causal relationship, we introduce Anti Adversarial Training (AT-AT), a training regime that intentionally learns non-robust features to obtain both superior reconstruction defense and higher accuracy than state-of-the-art defenses. Our results revise the prevailing understanding of training data exposure and reveal a new privacy-robustness tradeoff.

</details>


### [132] [Same Loss, Same Noise, Opposite Schedules: Noise Structure and Optimizer Normalization Jointly Determine Whether Learning-Rate Cooldown Helps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12360)
*Subham Singh,Ashutosh Mishra,Subha Raut*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对WSD学习率调度中的冷却阶段进行了理论分析，证明其效果取决于梯度噪声结构与优化器是否归一化更新步长：在乘法噪声下，SGD无需冷却也能收敛，而符号/归一化方法（如自适应优化器）则必须冷却才能降低噪声底限。作者通过解析求解signSGD稳态分布、扩展至高维归一化SGD等，提供了完整的理论框架和实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模模型预训练中广泛使用的WSD学习率调度中，冷却阶段有时显著降低最终训练损失，有时则无效，其背后的条件尚不明确。本文旨在给出一个可证明的解释，阐明何种情况下冷却有效、何种情况下无效。

Method: 在强凸目标下，分别考虑乘法（梯度比例）噪声和加性噪声，对比SGD与符号/归一化方法的收敛行为。通过解析求解signSGD在二次型上的稳态分布得到闭式底限常数；利用$(L_0,L_1)$-光滑性证明局部形式的解耦；通过尺度不变性论证将底限扩展至d>1维的归一化SGD；并验证对动量和重尾噪声的鲁棒性。

Result: 1) 乘法噪声下，SGD以常数学习率几何收缩，冷却无增益；2) 同条件下，符号/归一化方法会停留在$η^2$量级的噪声底限，只有将学习率驱零才能达到最优；3) 加性噪声会为所有方法引入底限；4) 机制核心：SGD步长随梯度缩放而自退火，归一化步长恒为单位尺度无法自退火；5) 在真实分类任务上验证了噪声机制诊断的有效性。

Conclusion: 冷却阶段是否有效取决于梯度噪声类型和优化器是否归一化；实际大规模训练中使用的内部冷却分数超出了稳态景观-噪声几何的范畴，需要进一步研究。

Abstract: The cooldown phase of a warmup-stable-decay (WSD) learning-rate schedule, now a default in large-model pretraining, lowers the final training loss in some settings and does nothing in others. We give a provable account of which case obtains, and it turns on two properties together: the structure of the gradient noise and whether the optimizer normalizes its update. On a strongly convex objective with multiplicative (gradient-proportional) noise, stochastic gradient descent contracts geometrically at a constant learning rate, so cooldown has nothing to improve. Under the same objective and noise, sign-based and normalized methods, the standard surrogates for adaptive optimizers, settle on a noise floor of order $η^2$ and reach the minimizer only as the learning rate is driven to zero; any additive noise then reinstates a floor for every method. The mechanism is elementary: an SGD step shrinks in proportion to the gradient and so anneals itself, whereas a normalized step keeps unit scale and cannot. We solve the signSGD stationary law on the quadratic exactly and obtain the floor constant in closed form, prove a local form of the dissociation under $(L_0,L_1)$-smoothness, extend the floor to normalized SGD in dimension d>1 by a scale-invariance argument, and establish robustness to momentum and heavy-tailed noise. Simulation confirms every prediction, and we demonstrate the resulting noise-regime diagnostic on a real classification task with directly measured gradient noise. The mechanism explains whether cooldown helps; the interior cooldown fraction used at scale lies outside stationary landscape-and-noise geometry.

</details>


### [133] [SinAE: A Single-Architecture Flow-Matching Autoencoder for Cross-Domain Atomic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12380)
*Yuxuan Ren,Fan Yang,Jianhua Yao,Yatao Bian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SinAE是一个基于流匹配的单一架构自编码器，使用普通Transformer（无等变、图或特定域算子）对分子、晶体和蛋白质进行近无损重建与生成，通过共享原子潜变量实现跨域迁移，在多项基准上达到强性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小分子、晶体和蛋白质均以3D空间中的原子为基本单元，但现有生成流程因使用不同的图、等变或框架架构而碎片化，跨域训练可缓解各领域数据稀缺问题，但直接3D坐标生成难以处理异构结构先验，且已有潜自编码器无法同时实现无损重构和跨架构通用性。

Method: 提出SinAE，采用单一架构的流匹配自编码器，编码器和解码器均为普通Transformer，不含等变、图或领域特定算子；将重建负担转移到迭代流匹配解码器上，而非要求编码器捕捉细粒度几何信息，实现近无损重建；每个token的潜变量支持标准的扩散Transformer先验。

Result: 相比已有的潜变量基线方法，重建误差降低数个数量级；在分子、晶体和蛋白质生成基准上达到强性能；分子-晶体联合训练严格提升了两个领域的表现，提供了跨域迁移的直接证据。

Conclusion: SinAE证明了使用单一普通Transformer架构、通过共享原子潜变量可实现分子、晶体和蛋白质的跨域生成与迁移，为统一3D原子级生成框架提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Small molecules, crystals, and proteins all reduce to atoms in 3D space, yet their generative pipelines remain fragmented across domains, each with its Small molecules, crystals, and proteins all reduce to atoms in 3D space, yet their generative pipelines remain fragmented across domains, each with its own graph, equivariant, or frame-based architecture. Cross-domain training would mitigate per-domain data scarcity, but direct generation in 3D coordinate space cannot easily handle the heterogeneous structural priors of all three domains, and no prior latent autoencoder is simultaneously lossless and architecturally general across all three. We introduce SinAE, a single-architecture flow-matching autoencoder for molecules, crystals, and proteins, with vanilla Transformer encoder and decoder and no equivariant, graph, or domain-specific operators. Rather than requiring the encoder to capture fine-grained geometry, SinAE shifts the reconstruction burden into an iterative flow-matching decoder, achieving near-lossless reconstruction across domains and reducing reconstruction errors by orders of magnitude relative to prior latent baselines. The same per-token latent supports a standard Diffusion Transformer prior that reaches strong performance on molecular, crystal, and protein generation benchmarks. Joint molecule--crystal training strictly improves both domains, providing direct evidence of cross-domain transfer through a shared atomic latent. Code is available at https://github.com/BlueWhaleLab/SinAE .

</details>


### [134] [Differentiable Clone-Structured Causal Graphs for End-to-End Cognitive Map Learning from Image Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12382)
*Arash Nikzad,Sasan Sarbishegi,Ali Dasmeh,Muhammad Asif,Parsa Gharavi,Erik Husom,Sagar Sen,Andrew B. Lehr,Olivier Penacchio,Ana Clemente,Tristan M. Stöber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出gradCSCG，将Clone-Structured Causal Graph算法（海马体规范模型）改造为全可微模块，并与VQ-VAE感知前端结合，实现了从原始图像序列中端到端学习环境的结构化因果图，解决了原算法依赖预定义离散符号且难以集成神经网络的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CSCG算法虽能从混淆观测中学习可解释的环境地图，但其需要预定义的离散符号集，且期望最大化形式难以与神经网络模块结合，无法实现从原始图像序列的端到端处理。本文旨在消除这一障碍。

Method: 将CSCG重构为全可微模块gradCSCG：引入软发射前向传播使地图学习目标能反向传播到感知模块；耦合VQ-VAE感知前端实现从原始图像输入；设计损失平衡机制缓解联合训练中的模块崩溃问题。

Result: 在原始符号网格世界上，梯度训练重现了CSCG的结果（成功恢复房间拓扑）；在MNIST图像序列环境中，每次访问位置都得到新采样的数字图像，地图恢复依然鲁棒；在四个高度混淆环境中，端到端流水线成功从视觉输入中恢复底层邻接图，边缘精确率和召回率均高。

Conclusion: 本文提供了CSCG可作为深度学习架构中可组合构建块的原理验证，证明了基于梯度的训练能够实现从原始感知到结构化因果图端到端学习。

Abstract: How can an agent build a structured map of its world from nothing but an ongoing sequence of raw sensory input and its own movements, especially when natural variation means exact sensory patterns rarely repeat? The Clone-Structured Causal Graph algorithm (CSCG), a normative hippocampus model, shows how an interpretable map can be learned from aliased observations. However, CSCG requires a predefined discrete alphabet, and its expectation-maximization formulation is not easily combined with existing neural network modules, preventing the end-to-end processing of raw image sequences. We remove this barrier by reformulating CSCG as a single, fully differentiable module, gradCSCG, and coupling it to a learned vector-quantized variational autoencoder (VQ-VAE) perceptual front-end. A soft emission forward pass allows the map-learning objective to flow back into perception, while a set of loss-balancing mechanisms mitigates module collapse during joint training. We demonstrate, first, that gradient training reproduces CSCG's results on original symbolic grid worlds by recovering room topology from heavily aliased observations. Second, we show that map recovery remains robust on MNIST image sequences, where each visit to a location yields a newly sampled image of its assigned digit. Across four heavily aliased environments, the end-to-end pipeline successfully uncovers the underlying adjacency graph with high edge precision and recall, directly from visual input. This work provides a proof of principle that CSCG can serve as a composable building block in a deep learning architecture.

</details>


### [135] [ReDiTT: Retrieval Augmented Conditional Diffusion Transformers for Asynchronous Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12391)
*Saiyue Lyu,Zhitian Zhang,Ruizhi Deng,Thibaut Durand*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ReDiTT是一种检索增强的条件扩散Transformer模型，用于异步时间序列预测，在潜在空间中通过检索结构相似的序列作为参考条件，在7个真实数据集上实现了最先进的预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异步时间序列中未来事件具有内在不确定性，现有方法在长期预测中稳定性不足且样本多样性有限。

Method: 提出ReDiTT模型，采用潜在空间扩散架构，引入检索增强机制从记忆库中提取结构相似的潜在序列，通过交叉注意力将其作为参考条件注入模型，实现全局结构引导生成。

Result: 在7个真实世界数据集上，在下一事件预测和长期预测任务中均达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: 检索增强的条件扩散模型能够有效稳定长期预测并提升样本多样性，验证了检索机制在异步时序预测中的有效性。

Abstract: We present a diffusion based model for asynchronous time series prediction, where the goal is to predict the next inter event time and event type. To address the inherent uncertainty of future events, we introduce ReDiTT, a retrieval augmented conditional diffusion transformer that operates in latent space. ReDiTT retrieves structurally similar latent sequences from a memory bank during both training and inference and incorporates them as reference conditions through cross attention. This retrieval based conditioning allows the model to attend to relevant temporal dynamics and provides global structural guidance for generation. As a result, ReDiTT stabilizes long horizon forecasting and improves sample diversity. Experiments on seven real world datasets demonstrate state of the art performance on next event prediction and long horizon forecasting. Our code is available at https://github.com/BorealisAI/ReDiTT.

</details>


### [136] [Mechanical Analysis of Parachute Suspension Line Deployment with Binding Tapes Using PINN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12409)
*Xiang Zhao,Ronghui Quan,Yaqi Xiao,Junlin Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于物理信息神经网络(PINN)的算法，用于预测降落伞吊带拉伸矫直过程中的张力，相比传统数值积分方法在计算效率和精度上均有提升，并通过飞行数据验证了其可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降落伞吊带拉伸矫直过程是开伞的关键初始阶段，涉及复杂的动态载荷变化，现有基于常微分方程数值积分的方法无法快速获取任意位置的张力值。

Method: 开发了物理信息神经网络(PINN)算法，将物理方程嵌入神经网络训练过程，实现吊带张力的快速精确预测。

Result: PINN算法在计算效率和数值精度上均优于传统积分方法，并揭示了绑带参数对吊带动态张力的调控规律，飞行试验数据验证了其可靠性。

Conclusion: PINN框架为降落伞吊带张力预测提供了更高效、精确的解决方案，有助于提升开伞过程的控制与安全性。

Abstract: Parachutes are widely utilized in aviation, aerospace and lifesaving missions. As the initial stage of parachute deployment, suspension line extraction and straightening directly determines the smooth implementation of subsequent inflation procedures. This ultra-short process involves intricate dynamic load variations. Most existing studies adopt numerical integration of ordinary differential equations to calculate line tension, yet this method fails to rapidly acquire tension values at arbitrary positions along suspension lines. This paper develops a physics-informed neural network (PINN) algorithm for tension prediction during line extraction and straightening, which outperforms traditional integration methods in both computational efficiency and numerical accuracy. Furthermore, the regulatory law of binding tape parameters on line dynamic tension is investigated. Comparative validations against flight test data and conventional numerical results verify the reliability and effectiveness of the proposed PINN framework.

</details>


### [137] [The Computational Basis of Confidence in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12447)
*Dharshan Kumaran,Viorica Patraucean,Maks Ovsanikov,Petar Veličković,Nathaniel Daw*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用统计决策置信度（SDC）框架研究多模态语言模型中答案对数几率（answer logits）是否反映潜在决策变量（即规范性置信度）而非启发式偏好信号。实验表明，在感知判别和记忆决策任务中，答案对数几率差满足SDC的定性特征（包括关键的折叠X模式），表明其作为潜在决策变量的单调读取器；但在复杂视觉推理中，完整几何特征缺失，揭示了当前框架的边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要从置信度预测正确性的准确性和校准性来评估模型置信度，但未回答更根本的问题：置信度信号本身究竟代表什么——是反映足以计算规范性置信度的潜在决策变量，还是仅仅是一种非贝叶斯方式的启发式偏好信号？作者希望从计算神经科学的规范性框架出发，厘清答案对数几率信号的本质。

Method: 采用计算神经科学中的统计决策置信度（SDC）框架，将答案对数几率差（LD）视为潜在决策变量的候选读取器。在三个感知判别任务、一个记忆决策任务以及复杂视觉推理任务上，测试LD是否满足SDC预测的定性特征（包括折叠X模式），涵盖三个多模态非推理模型和一个推理模型。

Result: 在感知判别和记忆决策任务中，LD满足SDC的所有定性特征（包括诊断性的正确/错误折叠X模式），表明答案对数几率表现为潜在决策变量的单调读取器而非启发式偏好分数。在复杂视觉推理中，LD仍能预测正确性并超越客观任务难度，但SDC的完整几何特征缺失。

Conclusion: 答案对数几率在多模态语言模型的感知和记忆决策中可作为规范性置信度的信号读取器，但在复杂推理中受到限制。SDC框架为跨生物智能和人工智能的置信度研究提供了统一的理论基础。

Abstract: Reliable confidence -- the probability that a model's own answer is correct -- is essential for the trustworthy deployment of language models. Existing work has largely evaluated confidence by how well it predicts correctness and whether it is calibrated, leaving open a more fundamental question: what does the confidence signal itself represent? Answer logits may reflect a latent decision variable sufficient to compute normative confidence, or instead a heuristic preference signal that combines the available evidence in a non-Bayesian manner. We address this using statistical decision confidence (SDC), a normative framework from computational neuroscience. Treating the answer-logit difference (LD) as a candidate readout of the latent decision variable, we test the qualitative signatures predicted by SDC. Across three perceptual discrimination tasks and a memory-based decision task, spanning three multimodal non-reasoning models and one reasoning model, LD satisfied these signatures -- including the diagnostic correct/error folded-X pattern -- showing that, in these settings, answer logits behave as monotonic readouts of a latent decision variable rather than heuristic preference scores. In complex visual reasoning, LD continued to predict correctness beyond objective task difficulty, but the full geometric signatures of SDC were absent, illustrating the current boundary of the framework when explicit normative process models are unavailable. These results provide a computational account of confidence in multimodal language models, delineate when answer logits behave as readouts of a latent decision variable, and establish SDC as a unifying framework for studying confidence across biological and artificial intelligence.

</details>


### [138] [Exploring Zero-Shot Foundation Models for Multivariate Time Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12454)
*Martin Uray,Saverio Messineo,Roland Kwitt,Stefan Huber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探究了将单变量时间序列预测基础模型TimesFM零样本应用于多变量时间序列异常检测（MTSAD）的效果。在SWaT基准上的实验表明，无论是作为逐特征预测器还是嵌入器，该模型均无法与已有基线方法竞争，原因是模型过于有效地捕捉了时间动态，导致异常窗口内预测误差过低，无法区分异常。但模型在异常边界处误差峰值明显，表明其适用于变化点检测而非MTSAD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MTSAD方法依赖应用特定模型，训练成本高且难以扩展。近期出现的时间序列基础模型（如TimesFM）在单变量预测中展现出强零样本泛化能力，作者希望探究这类模型能否无需任务特定训练就能解决MTSAD问题。

Method: 以TimesFM为基础模型，在SWaT基准上测试两种零样本策略：（1）将FM作为逐特征预测器，基于阈值化预测误差进行异常检测；（2）将FM作为嵌入器，利用其中间表示输入标准离群点检测器进行异常识别。

Result: 两种策略均无法与已有基线方法竞争。嵌入表示仅能部分分离正常与异常片段，不足以实现可靠检测。模型在完全异常窗口内预测误差极低，导致持续异常无法被区分。但误差在异常边界处出现峰值。

Conclusion: 所提出的朴素零样本FM方法不适用于MTSAD，但模型在变化点检测方面具有潜力，可用于检测分布变化。

Abstract: Multivariate Time Series Anomaly Detection (MTSAD) is essential for reliability and safety in domains such as industrial process monitoring and financial risk management, yet conventional approaches rely on application-specific models that are costly to train and hard to scale. Foundation Models (FMs), pre-trained on broad data with strong zero-shot generalization, have recently become available for univariate time series forecasting, raising the question of whether they can address MTSAD without task-specific training. We investigate the zero-shot application of a univariate forecasting FM, TimesFM, to industrial MTSAD on the Secure Water Treatment (SWaT) benchmark, evaluating two strategies: treating the FM as a per-feature forecaster with thresholded prediction errors, and as an embedder whose intermediate representations feed standard outlier detectors. Neither of our proposed setups is competitive with established baselines; embeddings reveal only partial separation between normal and anomalous segments, insufficient for reliable detection. The cause is that the FM is too effective at capturing temporal dynamics, yielding low error even within fully anomalous windows, so persistent anomalies become indistinguishable from normal behavior. However, these observations yield valuable insights: the error peaks at anomaly boundaries, indicating FMs reliably detect distribution changes. We conclude that the proposed naive zero-shot FMs are unsuitable for MTSAD but promising for change-point detection.

</details>


### [139] [Sample Efficient Generative Optimization for Molecular Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12488)
*Sarina Kopf,Cristina Nevado,Philippe Schwaller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SEGO是一种样本高效的分子优化框架，结合贝叶斯优化与自适应生成模型，在仅使用其他方法十分之一评估次数的情况下达到SOTA性能，并在多参数对接任务中约一半调用次数内找到十个命中物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药物发现、材料设计和催化中的分子优化需要在紧预算下搜索庞大化学空间，高保真预测和实验测量成本高昂，亟需提升优化方法的样本效率。

Method: SEGO框架包含四个步骤：概率代理模型对化学空间中可能存在命中物的区域形成假设；生成模型被引导在该区域提出候选分子；通过采集函数选择最有希望的候选；将实际预言机调用结果同时用于精炼代理模型和锚定生成器的奖励信号。

Result: 在PMO基准上，SEGO仅使用其他方法十分之一的预言机调用次数即达到SOTA性能；在多参数对接任务中，SEGO使用约一半的调用次数即可找到十个命中物。

Conclusion: SEGO显著提升了分子优化的样本效率，使得该方法更接近直接由实验反馈驱动的实际应用场景。

Abstract: Molecular optimization in drug discovery, materials design, and catalysis requires searching vast chemical spaces under tight evaluation budgets, since high-fidelity oracles and experimental measurements are costly. The practical impact of an optimization method therefore hinges on its sample efficiency: how few evaluations it needs to find strong candidates. We introduce Sample Efficient Generative Optimization (SEGO), a framework for Bayesian optimization on adaptively generated molecules. In SEGO, a probabilistic surrogate model forms a hypothesis about where hits lie in chemical space, a generative model is steered to propose candidates in that region, the most promising candidate is selected via an acquisition function, and the resulting oracle call is used both to sharpen the surrogate and to anchor the generator in real reward. SEGO attains state-of-the-art performance on the practical molecular optimization (PMO) benchmark using only one tenth of the oracle calls consumed by other methods, and on a multiparameter docking task it reaches ten hits in roughly half the oracle calls of existing approaches. These gains move molecular optimization closer to campaigns driven by direct experimental feedback.

</details>


### [140] [Adversarial Attacks on Online Handwriting using Salience-based Temporal Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12500)
*Yataro Tamura,Brian Kenji Iwana,Jiseok Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于显著性引导的时间编辑攻击框架，通过对在线手写轨迹的关键时间点进行插删点操作生成对抗样本，在保持笔迹视觉结构的同时实现更强的单次黑盒迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对图像输入的对抗攻击方法（加性空间扰动）应用于在线手写识别时会产生高频抖动和不自然的笔划伪影，亟需一种尊重手写时序特性的对抗攻击方法。

Method: 通过梯度激活映射估计时间显著性，选择对原始类别预测贡献最大的时间步，在这些步上插入或删除点来生成对抗样本，而非添加噪声。

Result: 在Unipen和CASIA-OLHWDB数据集上，传统图像攻击白盒性能强但跨模型迁移性差；所提时间编辑攻击在保持笔迹视觉结构的同时，实现了更强的单次黑盒迁移性。

Conclusion: 时间编辑攻击是在线手写识别中一种相关威胁模型，尤其在单次黑盒迁移场景下具有优势。

Abstract: Deep learning models for online handwriting recognition have been shown effective and are increasingly deployed in practical applications. However, their vulnerability to adversarial attacks is still a challenge. Existing adversarial methods are predominantly designed for image-based inputs and typically rely on additive spatial perturbations. When applied to online handwriting, which is inherently represented as a time series of pen trajectories, such perturbations often introduce high-frequency jitter and visibly unnatural stroke artifacts. In this work, we propose a novel adversarial attack framework for online handwriting recognition based on salience-guided temporal editing. Instead of adding noise, the proposed method generates adversarial examples by inserting and deleting points at time steps selected according to temporal salience, preserving the shape and smoothness of the original handwriting. Temporal salience is estimated using gradient-based activation mapping, which guides edits toward time steps that strongly support the original class prediction. We evaluate the proposed approach on the Unipen and CASIA-OLHWDB datasets under both white-box and one-shot black-box attack settings. Experimental results demonstrate that while conventional image-based attacks achieve strong white-box performance, they exhibit poor transferability across models. In contrast, the proposed temporal editing attack achieves stronger one-shot black-box transferability while preserving the visual structure of the handwriting. These results indicate that temporal editing is a relevant threat model for online handwriting recognition, particularly in one-shot black-box transfer settings.

</details>


### [141] [OOD-RL-Bench: A Benchmark Framework for Out-of-Distribution Detection in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12523)
*Emil Mittag,Richard Dazeley,Peter Vamplew*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了OOD-RL-Bench框架，用于评估强化学习轨迹中的分布外检测器，实验发现观测扰动和状态切换检测准确率高，而观测延迟和动作条件动力学检测困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的OOD检测基准通常评估图像分类器或静态低维数据集，未能考虑RL轨迹中复杂的、动作依赖的时序结构，因此需要专门的评估框架。

Method: 设计了OOD-RL-Bench框架，通过共享接口和配置集成检测器与异常注入器，使用DQN策略在LunarLander-v3环境中评估，采用匹配时间AUROC、AUPRC、假阳性率、检测延迟和分段起始指标。

Result: 不同异常类型下检测性能差异显著：观测扰动和状态切换检测准确率高，而观测延迟和动作条件动力学检测困难，即使将异常发生后得分与同时间步的干净得分对比也如此。

Conclusion: OOD-RL-Bench为评估RL轨迹OOD检测提供了可扩展的统一框架，公开了框架、策略检查点和完整结果作为可复现制品。

Abstract: Reliable reinforcement learning (RL) agents must maintain operational integrity amidst sensor malfunctions, dynamic disturbances, and slow environmental shifts. The detection of out-of-distribution conditions is pivotal to determining when an agent's observations, transitions, or trajectory dynamics deviate from the assumptions underpinning its policy training. Current out-of-distribution (OOD) detection benchmarks typically evaluate image classifiers or static low-dimensional datasets, failing to account for the complex, action-dependent temporal structure inherent in RL trajectories. To address this gap, we present OOD-RL-Bench, a comprehensive and extensible framework designed to evaluate OOD detectors against categories of anomalies injected into RL trajectories. Detectors and anomaly injectors are integrated through shared interfaces and configuration, which allows new scoring methods and perturbation families to be evaluated without modification of the core benchmark loop. We evaluate the utility of the framework using a Deep Q-Network policy within the LunarLander-v3 environment. We assess the performance of each detector across a suite of anomaly types using matched-time AUROC, matched-time AUPRC, matched-time false-positive rate, detection delay, and segmented-onset metrics. Our analysis reveals significant performance variance across anomaly types: observation perturbations and regime switches are identified with high accuracy by several methods, while observation delay and action-conditioned dynamics remain difficult even when post-onset anomaly scores are compared against clean scores from the same timesteps. We make the framework, trained policy checkpoint, and complete results publicly available as a reproducible artefact.

</details>


### [142] [From Preimage Search To Source-Grounded Feature Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12526)
*Kaixiang Shu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出源接地特征反演方法，通过Wiener滤波器修复计算图边界处的反向传播信号，实现无需查询优化的单次反向传播特征可视化，支持多种架构和张量组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准特征反演方法通过目标匹配寻找输入，但存在解的非唯一性问题，无法确定与生成特征的原始样本相关联的逆映射。需要一种能够考虑源样本局部网络几何特性的反演方法。

Method: 在计算DAG的每个边界处，利用反向传播提供正确反向依赖关系，通过闭式矩阵Wiener映射从均值种子VJP修复信号到上游状态，再通过第二个Wiener映射处理JVP前向一致性残差，将修复后的状态通过同一DAG进行一次有限反向传播。该方法支持零截距映射家族，适用于多种架构。

Result: 该方法无需查询特定优化，适用于CNN、Transformer等多种架构及不同张量组件和视觉分布。匹配的目标和源控制实验验证了逆映射依赖于所选特征和被解释样本的局部算子，而非与目标无关的图像模板。预测条件特征图谱将可视化结果与对应内部特征的独立干预对齐。

Conclusion: 源接地特征反演打开了模型隐藏的特征层次结构，允许在单层和通道级别进行检查，连接了网络从输入提取的内容与塑造其决策的内部证据。

Abstract: Interpreting a neural network requires understanding what its internal features extract from a particular input. Feature inversion seeks to express a selected feature in the input domain, but canonical iterative methods search for an input whose re-encoded representation matches the target.
